# Cheiron Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** A backend that turns a natural-language clinical-trials question into a cited visualization spec backed by live ClinicalTrials.gov data, plus a small web UI that renders it.

**Architecture:** A judged pipeline. Jev (TypeSafe) answers every closed decision about the question in one request; a fast OpenAI model extracts the named strings in parallel; deterministic code plans the ClinicalTrials.gov requests, fetches projected study rows, aggregates, cites, and emits a zod-validated spec. Fastify exposes `POST /query` (JSON) and `POST /query/stream` (SSE); a Vite + React app renders the spec.

**Tech Stack:** Node 24, TypeScript 5.7, npm workspaces, zod 4, Fastify 5, vitest 3, eslint 9, tsx, Vite 7, React 19, Recharts 3, D3 7, topojson-client, world-atlas, i18n-iso-countries, `@fontsource-variable/schibsted-grotesk`.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-09-cheiron-design.md`

## Global Constraints

- Node `>= 22`, developed on 24. ESM everywhere (`"type": "module"`). `moduleResolution: "bundler"`, extensionless relative imports.
- Secrets only from `.env` (`TYPESAFE_API_KEY`, `OPENAI_API_KEY`); never log a key, never read `api_key/`.
- Jev endpoint `POST https://api.typesafe.ai/v1/systemone`, body `{ state, model: "jev-latest", questions }`, bearer auth. State ≤ 32k tokens. A choice has 2–255 options.
- ClinicalTrials.gov base `https://clinicaltrials.gov/api/v2`. Fields list is exactly the 19 in Task 6. `pageSize=1000`, `sort=StartDate:desc`, `countTotal=true` on the first page only.
- Every response validates against `QueryResponse` from `@cheiron/contracts` before it leaves the API. Unknown request fields are rejected (400).
- Multi-valued fields count a study once per distinct value, and the note is emitted whenever that happens.
- Chart decisions follow the compatibility matrix in Task 10 exactly; no chart is applied outside it.
- Clients never throw: Jev, OpenAI and ClinicalTrials.gov failures are typed values mapped to 502/504.
- Commits: one declarative sentence, present tense, ending with `Co-Authored-By: Claude Fable 5.1 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.
- UI: white only, `--brand: #1f4fff`, Schibsted Grotesk, no dark-mode tokens.

## Review Focus

1. A question that names no entity at all ("How are trials distributed across phases?") must still run: every `query.*` parameter is omitted and the registry's full set is aggregated (truncated to `max_studies`, stated in the subtitle). Pinned in Task 10 (`plan` with empty entities) and Task 15.
2. A drug named in several interventions of one study ("Pembrolizumab 200 mg" and "pembrolizumab") must count once in a drug bucket and once as a graph node. Pinned in Task 9 (`drugIdentity` dedupes case-insensitively).
3. A study with no start date must not crash time bucketing or scatter, and must be counted in `coverage.studies_without_value`. Pinned in Task 11 and Task 12.
4. A ClinicalTrials.gov page that arrives with `nextPageToken` after `max_studies` is reached must stop fetching and set `truncated: true` with the registry's `totalCount`. Pinned in Task 7.
5. A Jev answer whose `probabilities` omit an option or do not sum to 1 must be rejected as `bad_response`, never read as a zero. Pinned in Task 4.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`packages/contracts/src/request.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { QueryRequest, asList } from "./request";

describe("QueryRequest", () => {
  it("applies defaults", () => {
    const r = QueryRequest.parse({ query: "  trials by phase " });
    expect(r).toEqual({ query: "trials by phase", max_studies: 3000, citations_per_datum: 5 });
  });

  it("rejects unknown fields", () => {
    expect(QueryRequest.safeParse({ query: "x", drug: "a" }).success).toBe(false);
  });

  it("rejects end_year before start_year", () => {
    expect(QueryRequest.safeParse({ query: "x", start_year: 2020, end_year: 2019 }).success).toBe(false);
  });

  it("accepts one or many entity values", () => {
    expect(QueryRequest.parse({ query: "x", drug_name: "a" }).drug_name).toBe("a");
    expect(QueryRequest.parse({ query: "x", drug_name: ["a", "b"] }).drug_name).toEqual(["a", "b"]);
    expect(QueryRequest.safeParse({ query: "x", drug_name: [] }).success).toBe(false);
  });

  it("bounds the caps", () => {
    expect(QueryRequest.safeParse({ query: "x", max_studies: 50 }).success).toBe(false);
    expect(QueryRequest.safeParse({ query: "x", citations_per_datum: 26 }).success).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("asList", () => {
  it("normalizes", () => {
    expect(asList(undefined)).toEqual([]);
    expect(asList("a")).toEqual(["a"]);
    expect(asList(["a", "b"])).toEqual(["a", "b"]);
  });
});
```

```ts
export const CHART_TYPES = [
  "bar", "grouped_bar", "stacked_bar", "line", "histogram", "scatter", "network_graph", "map", "table", "single_value",
] as const;
export type ChartType = (typeof CHART_TYPES)[number];

export const INTENTS = [
  "time_trend", "distribution", "comparison", "ranking", "geographic", "network", "count", "lookup", "other",
] as const;
export type Intent = (typeof INTENTS)[number];

export const DIMENSIONS = [
  "phase", "status", "sponsor_class", "sponsor", "intervention_type", "drug", "condition", "country",
  "start_year", "enrollment", "study_type", "sex", "age_group", "none",
] as const;
export type Dimension = (typeof DIMENSIONS)[number];

export const METRICS = ["trial_count", "enrollment_total", "enrollment_median"] as const;
export type Metric = (typeof METRICS)[number];

export const GRANULARITIES = ["year", "quarter", "month"] as const;
export type Granularity = (typeof GRANULARITIES)[number];

export const EDGE_KINDS = ["sponsor_drug", "drug_drug", "condition_drug", "sponsor_condition", "sponsor_collaborator"] as const;
export type EdgeKind = (typeof EDGE_KINDS)[number];

export const PHASES = ["EARLY_PHASE1", "PHASE1", "PHASE2", "PHASE3", "PHASE4", "NA"] as const;
export type Phase = (typeof PHASES)[number];

export const OVERALL_STATUSES = [
  "RECRUITING", "NOT_YET_RECRUITING", "ENROLLING_BY_INVITATION", "ACTIVE_NOT_RECRUITING", "COMPLETED",
  "SUSPENDED", "TERMINATED", "WITHDRAWN", "AVAILABLE", "NO_LONGER_AVAILABLE", "TEMPORARILY_NOT_AVAILABLE",
  "APPROVED_FOR_MARKETING", "WITHHELD", "UNKNOWN",
] as const;
export type OverallStatus = (typeof OVERALL_STATUSES)[number];

export const SPONSOR_CLASSES = ["INDUSTRY", "NIH", "FED", "OTHER_GOV", "NETWORK", "OTHER", "INDIV", "UNKNOWN", "AMBIG"] as const;
export type SponsorClass = (typeof SPONSOR_CLASSES)[number];

export const STUDY_TYPES = ["INTERVENTIONAL", "OBSERVATIONAL", "EXPANDED_ACCESS"] as const;
export type StudyType = (typeof STUDY_TYPES)[number];

export const SEXES = ["ALL", "FEMALE", "MALE"] as const;
export type Sex = (typeof SEXES)[number];

export const AGE_GROUPS = ["CHILD", "ADULT", "OLDER_ADULT"] as const;
export type AgeGroup = (typeof AGE_GROUPS)[number];

export const INTERVENTION_TYPES = [
  "DRUG", "BIOLOGICAL", "DEVICE", "PROCEDURE", "BEHAVIORAL", "RADIATION", "DIETARY_SUPPLEMENT", "GENETIC",
  "COMBINATION_PRODUCT", "DIAGNOSTIC_TEST", "OTHER",
] as const;
export type InterventionType = (typeof INTERVENTION_TYPES)[number];
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Request schema**

`packages/contracts/src/request.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from "zod";
import { CHART_TYPES, INTENTS, OVERALL_STATUSES, PHASES } from "./enums";

const term = z.string().trim().min(1).max(200);
const oneOrMany = z.union([term, z.array(term).min(1).max(10)]);
const year = z.number().int().min(1900).max(2100);

export const QueryRequest = z
  .object({
    query: z.string().trim().min(1).max(500),
    drug_name: oneOrMany.optional(),
    condition: oneOrMany.optional(),
    sponsor: oneOrMany.optional(),
    country: oneOrMany.optional(),
    trial_phase: z.enum(PHASES).optional(),
    status: z.enum(OVERALL_STATUSES).optional(),
    start_year: year.optional(),
    end_year: year.optional(),
    intent: z.enum(INTENTS).optional(),
    chart: z.enum(CHART_TYPES).optional(),
    max_studies: z.number().int().min(100).max(10_000).default(3000),
    citations_per_datum: z.number().int().min(0).max(25).default(5),
  })
  .strict()
  .refine((r) => r.start_year === undefined || r.end_year === undefined || r.end_year >= r.start_year, {
    message: "end_year must be greater than or equal to start_year",
    path: ["end_year"],
  });

export type QueryRequest = z.output<typeof QueryRequest>;
export type QueryRequestInput = z.input<typeof QueryRequest>;

/** The entity fields accept one value or several; the engine always wants a list. */
export const asList = (v: string | string[] | undefined): string[] => (v === undefined ? [] : Array.isArray(v) ? v : [v]);
```

`packages/contracts/src/index.ts`:

```ts
export const VERSION = "cheiron/0.1.0";
export * from "./enums";
export * from "./request";
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { QueryResponse, Visualization, ErrorBody } from "./response";
import { jsonSchemas } from "./json-schema";

const citation = { nct_id: "NCT01234567", title: "A study", field: "protocolSection.designModule.phases", excerpt: "PHASE3" };
const row = (extra: Record<string, unknown>) => ({ study_count: 1, citations: [citation], ...extra });

const meta = {
  query: "q",
  interpretation: {
    intent: { value: "distribution", p: 0.8, confidence: 0.7, alternatives: [{ value: "distribution", p: 0.8 }] },
    dimension: { value: "phase", p: 0.9 },
    secondary_dimension: null,
    chart: { chosen: "bar", applied: "bar", explicit: false, reason: null },
    metric: "trial_count", granularity: null, edge_kind: null,

    time_window: { start_year: null, end_year: null },
    limit: null, recruiting_only: false, combination: null, provided_fields: [],
    question_set: { id: "query-interpretation", version: 1 },
    models: { judge: "jev-latest", extractor: "gpt-5.4-nano", titler: "gpt-5.4-nano" },
  },
  arms: [{ label: "pembrolizumab", filters: { "query.intr": "\"pembrolizumab\"" } }],
  source: { name: "clinicaltrials.gov", api_version: "2.0.5", data_timestamp: "2026-10-08T09:00:05", requests: [{ url: "https://x", total_count: 10, fetched: 10 }] },
  coverage: { total_count: 10, fetched_count: 10, truncated: false, sorted_by: "StartDate:desc", studies_without_value: 0, omitted_categories: 0 },
  units: { y: "trials" }, granularity: null,
  timing: { jev_ms: 1, extract_ms: 1, fetch_ms: 1, title_ms: 1, aggregate_ms: 1, total_ms: 5 },
  notes: [], warnings: [], version: "cheiron/0.1.0",
};

describe("Visualization", () => {
  it("accepts a bar spec", () => {
    const v = {
      type: "bar", title: "t", subtitle: "s",
      encoding: { x: { field: "phase", type: "ordinal", label: "Phase", order: ["PHASE1"] }, y: { field: "trial_count", type: "quantitative", label: "Trials", unit: "trials" }, orientation: "vertical", sort: "x_order" },
      data: [row({ phase: "PHASE1", trial_count: 3 })],
    };
    expect(Visualization.safeParse(v).success).toBe(true);
  });

  it("accepts a network spec", () => {
    const v = {
      type: "network_graph", title: "t", subtitle: "s",
      encoding: { nodes: { id_field: "id", label_field: "label", kind_field: "kind", size_field: "size", kinds: [{ kind: "sponsor", label: "Sponsor" }] }, edges: { source_field: "source", target_field: "target", weight_field: "weight" }, layout: "force" },
      data: { nodes: [{ id: "sponsor:merck", label: "Merck", kind: "sponsor", size: 3, study_count: 3 }], edges: [{ source: "sponsor:merck", target: "drug:x", weight: 2, study_count: 2, citations: [citation] }] },
    };
    expect(Visualization.safeParse(v).success).toBe(true);
  });

  it("rejects a single_value with two rows", () => {
    const v = { type: "single_value", title: "t", subtitle: "s", encoding: { value: { field: "value", label: "Trials", unit: "trials" } }, data: [row({ value: 1, label: "a" }), row({ value: 2, label: "b" })] };
    expect(Visualization.safeParse(v).success).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("QueryResponse", () => {
  it("accepts a null visualization with meta", () => {
    expect(QueryResponse.safeParse({ visualization: null, meta }).success).toBe(true);
  });
  it("exports JSON Schema for request and response", () => {
    const s = jsonSchemas();
    expect(s.request).toHaveProperty("properties.query");
    expect(s.response).toHaveProperty("properties.visualization");
  });
  it("shapes errors", () => {
    expect(ErrorBody.safeParse({ error: { code: "not_answerable", message: "m" } }).success).toBe(true);
    expect(ErrorBody.safeParse({ error: { code: "nope", message: "m" } }).success).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Citation and data row**

`packages/contracts/src/citation.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from "zod";

/** One registry record that supports a datum, with the exact value that put it there. */
export const Citation = z.object({
  nct_id: z.string().regex(/^NCT\d{8}$/),
  title: z.string(),
  /** JSON path in the ClinicalTrials.gov response, e.g. protocolSection.designModule.phases */
  field: z.string(),
  /** The exact value at that path (or the two values, for an edge). */
  excerpt: z.string(),
});
export type Citation = z.infer<typeof Citation>;

const cell = z.union([z.string(), z.number(), z.null()]);

/** Every data row carries how many studies it stands for and which ones. */
export const DataRow = z.object({ study_count: z.number().int().nonnegative(), citations: z.array(Citation) }).catchall(cell);
export type DataRow = z.infer<typeof DataRow>;
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Visualization union**

`packages/contracts/src/visualization.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from "zod";
import { Citation, DataRow } from "./citation";
import { GRANULARITIES } from "./enums";

const unit = z.enum(["trials", "participants"]);
const QuantAxis = z.object({ field: z.string(), type: z.literal("quantitative"), label: z.string(), unit });
const CatAxis = z.object({ field: z.string(), type: z.enum(["nominal", "ordinal"]), label: z.string(), order: z.array(z.string()).optional() });
const TemporalAxis = z.object({ field: z.string(), type: z.literal("temporal"), granularity: z.enum(GRANULARITIES), label: z.string() });
const Series = z.object({ field: z.string(), label: z.string(), values: z.array(z.string()) });
const head = { title: z.string(), subtitle: z.string() };

const barEncoding = z.object({ x: CatAxis, y: QuantAxis, orientation: z.enum(["vertical", "horizontal"]), sort: z.enum(["x_order", "value_desc"]) });

export const BarViz = z.object({ type: z.literal("bar"), ...head, encoding: barEncoding, data: z.array(DataRow) });
export const GroupedBarViz = z.object({ type: z.literal("grouped_bar"), ...head, encoding: barEncoding.extend({ series: Series }), data: z.array(DataRow) });
export const StackedBarViz = z.object({ type: z.literal("stacked_bar"), ...head, encoding: barEncoding.extend({ series: Series }), data: z.array(DataRow) });
export const LineViz = z.object({ type: z.literal("line"), ...head, encoding: z.object({ x: TemporalAxis, y: QuantAxis, series: Series.optional() }), data: z.array(DataRow) });
export const HistogramViz = z.object({
  type: z.literal("histogram"), ...head,
  encoding: z.object({ x: z.object({ field: z.string(), type: z.literal("ordinal"), label: z.string(), bins: z.array(z.object({ label: z.string(), min: z.number(), max: z.number().nullable() })) }), y: QuantAxis }),
  data: z.array(DataRow),
});
export const ScatterViz = z.object({
  type: z.literal("scatter"), ...head,
  encoding: z.object({
    x: z.object({ field: z.string(), type: z.enum(["temporal", "quantitative"]), label: z.string() }),
    y: z.object({ field: z.string(), type: z.literal("quantitative"), label: z.string(), unit }),
    color: z.object({ field: z.string(), label: z.string(), values: z.array(z.string()) }).optional(),
    series: Series.optional(),
    point_id: z.literal("nct_id"),
  }),
  data: z.array(DataRow),
});
export const NetworkViz = z.object({
  type: z.literal("network_graph"), ...head,
  encoding: z.object({
    nodes: z.object({ id_field: z.literal("id"), label_field: z.literal("label"), kind_field: z.literal("kind"), size_field: z.literal("size"), kinds: z.array(z.object({ kind: z.string(), label: z.string() })) }),
    edges: z.object({ source_field: z.literal("source"), target_field: z.literal("target"), weight_field: z.literal("weight") }),
    layout: z.literal("force"),
  }),
  data: z.object({
    nodes: z.array(z.object({ id: z.string(), label: z.string(), kind: z.string(), size: z.number(), study_count: z.number().int() })),
    edges: z.array(z.object({ source: z.string(), target: z.string(), weight: z.number(), study_count: z.number().int(), citations: z.array(Citation) })),
  }),
});
export const MapViz = z.object({
  type: z.literal("map"), ...head,
  encoding: z.object({ geo: z.object({ field: z.literal("country"), code_field: z.literal("iso3"), level: z.literal("country") }), value: z.object({ field: z.string(), label: z.string(), unit }) }),
  data: z.array(DataRow),
});
export const TableViz = z.object({
  type: z.literal("table"), ...head,
  encoding: z.object({ columns: z.array(z.object({ field: z.string(), label: z.string(), type: z.enum(["string", "number", "date", "list"]) })), sort: z.object({ field: z.string(), direction: z.enum(["asc", "desc"]) }) }),
  data: z.array(DataRow),
});
export const SingleValueViz = z.object({
  type: z.literal("single_value"), ...head,
  encoding: z.object({ value: z.object({ field: z.string(), label: z.string(), unit }) }),
  data: z.array(DataRow).length(1),
});

export const Visualization = z.discriminatedUnion("type", [
  BarViz, GroupedBarViz, StackedBarViz, LineViz, HistogramViz, ScatterViz, NetworkViz, MapViz, TableViz, SingleValueViz,
]);
export type Visualization = z.infer<typeof Visualization>;
export type BarViz = z.infer<typeof BarViz>;
export type GroupedBarViz = z.infer<typeof GroupedBarViz>;
export type StackedBarViz = z.infer<typeof StackedBarViz>;
export type LineViz = z.infer<typeof LineViz>;
export type HistogramViz = z.infer<typeof HistogramViz>;
export type ScatterViz = z.infer<typeof ScatterViz>;
export type NetworkViz = z.infer<typeof NetworkViz>;
export type MapViz = z.infer<typeof MapViz>;
export type TableViz = z.infer<typeof TableViz>;
export type SingleValueViz = z.infer<typeof SingleValueViz>;
```

```ts
import { z } from "zod";
import { CHART_TYPES, DIMENSIONS, EDGE_KINDS, GRANULARITIES, INTENTS, METRICS } from "./enums";

const judged = <T extends readonly [string, ...string[]]>(values: T) => z.object({ value: z.enum(values), p: z.number().min(0).max(1) });

export const Interpretation = z.object({
  intent: z.object({ value: z.enum(INTENTS), p: z.number(), confidence: z.number(), alternatives: z.array(judged(INTENTS)) }),
  dimension: judged(DIMENSIONS).nullable(),
  secondary_dimension: judged(DIMENSIONS).nullable(),
  chart: z.object({ chosen: z.enum(CHART_TYPES), applied: z.enum(CHART_TYPES).nullable(), explicit: z.boolean(), reason: z.string().nullable() }),
  metric: z.enum(METRICS),
  granularity: z.enum(GRANULARITIES).nullable(),
  edge_kind: z.enum(EDGE_KINDS).nullable(),
  entities: z.object({ drugs: z.array(z.string()), conditions: z.array(z.string()), sponsors: z.array(z.string()), countries: z.array(z.string()) }),
  time_window: z.object({ start_year: z.number().nullable(), end_year: z.number().nullable() }),
  limit: z.number().nullable(),
  recruiting_only: z.boolean(),
  combination: z.boolean().nullable(),
  provided_fields: z.array(z.string()),
  question_set: z.object({ id: z.string(), version: z.number().int() }),
  models: z.object({ judge: z.string(), extractor: z.string(), titler: z.string() }),
});
export type Interpretation = z.infer<typeof Interpretation>;

export const Meta = z.object({
  query: z.string(),
  interpretation: Interpretation,
  arms: z.array(z.object({ label: z.string(), filters: z.record(z.string(), z.string()) })),
  source: z.object({
    name: z.literal("clinicaltrials.gov"),
    api_version: z.string(),
    data_timestamp: z.string(),
    requests: z.array(z.object({ url: z.string(), total_count: z.number().int(), fetched: z.number().int() })),
  }),
  coverage: z.object({
    total_count: z.number().int(), fetched_count: z.number().int(), truncated: z.boolean(),
    sorted_by: z.literal("StartDate:desc"), studies_without_value: z.number().int(), omitted_categories: z.number().int(),
  }),
  units: z.object({ y: z.enum(["trials", "participants"]) }),
  granularity: z.enum(GRANULARITIES).nullable(),
  timing: z.object({ jev_ms: z.number(), extract_ms: z.number(), fetch_ms: z.number(), title_ms: z.number(), aggregate_ms: z.number(), total_ms: z.number() }),
  notes: z.array(z.string()),
  warnings: z.array(z.string()),
  version: z.string(),
});
export type Meta = z.infer<typeof Meta>;
```

```ts
import { QueryResponse } from "./response";

/** The same schemas, as JSON Schema, for GET /schema and the README. */
export function jsonSchemas(): { request: Record<string, unknown>; response: Record<string, unknown> } {

}
```

### Task 4: Judgment client (Jev over fetch), fake, and helpers

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/judgment/package.json`, `packages/judgment/src/types.ts`, `packages/judgment/src/client.ts`, `packages/judgment/src/fake.ts`, `packages/judgment/src/questions.ts`, `packages/judgment/src/index.ts`, `packages/judgment/src/client.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `JudgmentClient` (`enabled`, `ask(req) → JudgmentResult`), `HttpJudgmentClient`, `DisabledJudgmentClient`, `FakeJudgmentClient(script)`, `judgmentClientFromEnv(env)`, helpers `noul(instructions, criteria?)`, `choice(instructions, criteria)`, types `QuestionMap, AnswersFor, ChoiceAnswer, NoulAnswer, JsonValue, FailureReason, Usage`, and `topK(probabilities, k)`.

This package is adapted from the author's earlier Jev client. Keep the behaviour: one endpoint, per-attempt timeout, bounded retries with full jitter on 429/529/5xx and network errors, `retry-after` honoured, strict validation of every answer (missing option, unnormalized distribution, wrong type all become `bad_response`), and `ask` never throws.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { HttpJudgmentClient, parseAnswers } from "./client";
import { choice, noul } from "./questions";

const questions = { kind: choice("Which?", { a: null, b: null, none: "neither" }), yes: noul("Is it?") };

const response = (body: unknown, status = 200, headers: Record<string, string> = {}) =>
  new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: { "content-type": "application/json", ...headers } });

const good = { model: "jev-1", usage: { input_tokens: 10, output_tokens: 0 }, answers: {
  kind: { type: "choice", choice: "a", probabilities: { a: 0.7, b: 0.2, none: 0.1 }, confidence: 0.55 },
  yes: { type: "noul", noul: 0.9 },
} };

describe("parseAnswers", () => {
  it("accepts a well-formed answer set", () => {
    const r = parseAnswers(good, questions);
    expect(r.ok).toBe(true);
    if (r.ok) expect(r.answers.kind).toMatchObject({ type: "choice", choice: "a" });
  });
  it("rejects a distribution that omits an option", () => {
    const bad = { ...good, answers: { ...good.answers, kind: { ...good.answers.kind, probabilities: { a: 0.8, b: 0.2 } } } };
    expect(parseAnswers(bad, questions)).toMatchObject({ ok: false });
  });
  it("rejects a distribution that does not sum to 1", () => {
    const bad = { ...good, answers: { ...good.answers, kind: { ...good.answers.kind, probabilities: { a: 0.5, b: 0.5, none: 0.5 } } } };
    expect(parseAnswers(bad, questions)).toMatchObject({ ok: false });
  });
  it("rejects a wrong type", () => {
    const bad = { ...good, answers: { ...good.answers, yes: { type: "choice", choice: "a" } } };
    expect(parseAnswers(bad, questions)).toMatchObject({ ok: false });
  });
});

describe("HttpJudgmentClient", () => {
  it("sends state, model and questions with a bearer token", async () => {
    let seen: { url: string; init: RequestInit } | null = null;
    const client = new HttpJudgmentClient({ apiKey: "k", fetch: async (url, init) => { seen = { url: String(url), init: init! }; return response(good); } });
    const r = await client.ask({ state: { question: "q" }, questions });
    expect(r.ok).toBe(true);
    expect(seen!.url).toBe("https://api.typesafe.ai/v1/systemone");
    expect((seen!.init.headers as Record<string, string>).authorization).toBe("Bearer k");
    expect(JSON.parse(seen!.init.body as string)).toEqual({ state: { question: "q" }, model: "jev-latest", questions });
  });
  it("retries a 429 and honours retry-after, then succeeds", async () => {
    let calls = 0; const sleeps: number[] = [];
    const client = new HttpJudgmentClient({ apiKey: "k", sleep: async (ms) => { sleeps.push(ms); }, fetch: async () => (++calls === 1 ? response({}, 429, { "retry-after": "1" }) : response(good)) });
    const r = await client.ask({ state: "s", questions });
    expect(r.ok).toBe(true); expect(calls).toBe(2); expect(sleeps[0]).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1000);
  });
  it("does not retry a 401", async () => {
    let calls = 0;
    const client = new HttpJudgmentClient({ apiKey: "k", sleep: async () => {}, fetch: async () => { calls++; return response({}, 401); } });
    const r = await client.ask({ state: "s", questions });
    expect(r).toMatchObject({ ok: false, reason: "unauthorized" }); expect(calls).toBe(1);
  });
  it("gives up after the retries with the last reason", async () => {
    const client = new HttpJudgmentClient({ apiKey: "k", maxRetries: 1, sleep: async () => {}, fetch: async () => response({}, 503) });
    expect(await client.ask({ state: "s", questions })).toMatchObject({ ok: false, reason: "server_error" });
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Types**

`packages/judgment/src/types.ts`:

```ts
export type JsonValue = string | number | boolean | null | JsonValue[] | { [key: string]: JsonValue };
export type Instructions = string | JsonValue[] | { [key: string]: JsonValue };

export interface NoulQuestion { type: "noul"; instructions: Instructions; criteria?: { true: string; false: string } }
export interface ChoiceQuestion<O extends string = string> {
  type: "choice"; instructions: Instructions;
  /** Up to 255 options. `null` means the option's name says it all. */
  criteria: Record<O, string | { [key: string]: JsonValue } | null>;
}
export type Question = NoulQuestion | ChoiceQuestion;
export type QuestionMap = Record<string, Question>;

export interface NoulAnswer { type: "noul"; /** probability of yes; 0.5 is "cannot tell" */ noul: number }
export interface ChoiceAnswer<O extends string = string> {
  type: "choice"; choice: O; probabilities: Record<O, number>;
  /** How concentrated the distribution is: (p_max - 1/n) / (1 - 1/n). Not the odds of being right. */
  confidence: number;
}
export type Answer = NoulAnswer | ChoiceAnswer;
export type AnswerFor<Q extends Question> = Q extends NoulQuestion ? NoulAnswer : Q extends ChoiceQuestion<infer O> ? ChoiceAnswer<O> : never;
export type AnswersFor<Q extends QuestionMap> = { [K in keyof Q]: AnswerFor<Q[K]> };

export interface Usage { inputTokens: number; outputTokens: number; requests: number; costUsd: number }

export type FailureReason =
  | "disabled" | "state_too_large" | "timeout" | "network" | "rate_limited" | "overloaded"
  | "unauthorized" | "invalid_request" | "server_error" | "bad_response";

export type JudgmentResult<Q extends QuestionMap = QuestionMap> =
  | { ok: true; answers: AnswersFor<Q>; usage: Usage; model: string; ms: number }
  | { ok: false; reason: FailureReason; detail?: string; usage: Usage; ms: number };

export interface JudgmentRequest<Q extends QuestionMap = QuestionMap> { state: JsonValue; questions: Q; signal?: AbortSignal }

export interface JudgmentClient {
  readonly enabled: boolean;
  readonly model: string;
  /** Never throws. A failure is a value the caller decides what to do with. */
  ask<Q extends QuestionMap>(req: JudgmentRequest<Q>): Promise<JudgmentResult<Q>>;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Client**

`packages/judgment/src/client.ts`:

```ts
import type { Answer, AnswersFor, FailureReason, JsonValue, JudgmentClient, JudgmentRequest, JudgmentResult, Question, QuestionMap, Usage } from "./types";

export const USD_PER_MILLION_INPUT_TOKENS = 0.042;
export const MAX_STATE_TOKENS = 32_000;
export const costUsd = (inputTokens: number): number => (inputTokens * USD_PER_MILLION_INPUT_TOKENS) / 1_000_000;
/** Pessimistic: three characters per token keeps the estimate above the real count. */
export const estimateTokens = (value: JsonValue): number => Math.ceil((typeof value === "string" ? value : JSON.stringify(value)).length / 3);
export const NO_USAGE: Usage = { inputTokens: 0, outputTokens: 0, requests: 0, costUsd: 0 };
const addUsage = (a: Usage, b: Usage): Usage => ({ inputTokens: a.inputTokens + b.inputTokens, outputTokens: a.outputTokens + b.outputTokens, requests: a.requests + b.requests, costUsd: a.costUsd + b.costUsd });

export interface HttpJudgmentConfig {
  apiKey: string; baseUrl?: string; model?: string;
  timeoutMs?: number; maxRetries?: number; backoffMs?: number;
  fetch?: typeof fetch; sleep?: (ms: number) => Promise<void>; now?: () => number; random?: () => number;
}

const RETRYABLE: Record<number, FailureReason> = { 429: "rate_limited", 529: "overloaded" };
const FATAL: Record<number, FailureReason> = { 400: "invalid_request", 401: "unauthorized", 403: "unauthorized", 422: "invalid_request" };

export class HttpJudgmentClient implements JudgmentClient {
  readonly enabled = true;
  readonly model: string;
  private readonly url: string; private readonly timeoutMs: number; private readonly maxRetries: number; private readonly backoffMs: number;
  private readonly http: typeof fetch; private readonly sleep: (ms: number) => Promise<void>; private readonly now: () => number; private readonly random: () => number;

  constructor(private readonly cfg: HttpJudgmentConfig) {
    this.url = `${(cfg.baseUrl ?? "https://api.typesafe.ai").replace(/\/+$/, "")}/v1/systemone`;
    this.model = cfg.model ?? "jev-latest";
    this.timeoutMs = cfg.timeoutMs ?? 5_000; this.maxRetries = cfg.maxRetries ?? 2; this.backoffMs = cfg.backoffMs ?? 300;
    this.http = cfg.fetch ?? fetch; this.sleep = cfg.sleep ?? ((ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms)));
    this.now = cfg.now ?? Date.now; this.random = cfg.random ?? Math.random;
  }

  async ask<Q extends QuestionMap>(req: JudgmentRequest<Q>): Promise<JudgmentResult<Q>> {
    const started = this.now();
    let usage = NO_USAGE;
    const fail = (reason: FailureReason, detail?: string): JudgmentResult<Q> => ({ ok: false, reason, ...(detail ? { detail } : {}), usage, ms: this.now() - started });
    if (!Object.keys(req.questions).length) return fail("invalid_request", "no questions");
    if (estimateTokens(req.state) > MAX_STATE_TOKENS) return fail("state_too_large");
    const body = JSON.stringify({ state: req.state, model: this.model, questions: req.questions });
    let last: { reason: FailureReason; detail?: string } = { reason: "network" };
    for (let attempt = 0; attempt <= this.maxRetries; attempt++) {
      if (req.signal?.aborted) return fail("timeout", "aborted by caller");
      usage = addUsage(usage, { ...NO_USAGE, requests: 1 });
      const tried = await this.once(body, req.signal);
      if (tried.kind === "response") {
        const parsed = parseAnswers(tried.json, req.questions);
        if (!parsed.ok) return fail("bad_response", parsed.detail);
        usage = addUsage(usage, { inputTokens: parsed.inputTokens, outputTokens: parsed.outputTokens, requests: 0, costUsd: costUsd(parsed.inputTokens) });
        return { ok: true, answers: parsed.answers as AnswersFor<Q>, usage, model: parsed.model, ms: this.now() - started };
      }
      last = { reason: tried.reason, ...(tried.detail ? { detail: tried.detail } : {}) };
      if (!tried.retryable || attempt === this.maxRetries) break;
      const ceiling = this.backoffMs * 2 ** attempt;
      await this.sleep(Math.max(tried.retryAfterMs ?? 0, Math.round(ceiling * (0.5 + this.random() / 2))));
    }
    return fail(last.reason, last.detail);
  }

  private async once(body: string, outer?: AbortSignal): Promise<
    | { kind: "response"; json: unknown }
    | { kind: "failure"; reason: FailureReason; detail?: string; retryable: boolean; retryAfterMs?: number }
  > {
    const timeout = AbortSignal.timeout(this.timeoutMs);
    const signal = outer ? AbortSignal.any([outer, timeout]) : timeout;
    let res: Response;
    try {
      res = await this.http(this.url, { method: "POST", headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${this.cfg.apiKey}`, "content-type": "application/json" }, body, signal });
    } catch (e) {
      const timedOut = timeout.aborted || (e instanceof Error && e.name === "TimeoutError");
      return { kind: "failure", reason: timedOut ? "timeout" : "network", detail: String(e), retryable: !outer?.aborted };
    }
    if (res.ok) {
      try { return { kind: "response", json: await res.json() }; } catch (e) { return { kind: "failure", reason: "bad_response", detail: String(e), retryable: false }; }
    }
    const detail = await res.text().then((t) => t.slice(0, 300)).catch(() => "");
    const fatal = FATAL[res.status];
    if (fatal) return { kind: "failure", reason: fatal, detail, retryable: false };
    const retryAfter = Number(res.headers.get("retry-after"));
    return {
      kind: "failure", reason: RETRYABLE[res.status] ?? "server_error", detail, retryable: res.status === 429 || res.status >= 500,
      ...(Number.isFinite(retryAfter) && retryAfter > 0 ? { retryAfterMs: Math.min(retryAfter * 1000, 30_000) } : {}),
    };
  }
}

const probability = (n: unknown): n is number => typeof n === "number" && n >= 0 && n <= 1;
const isRecord = (v: unknown): v is Record<string, unknown> => typeof v === "object" && v !== null && !Array.isArray(v);
export const PROBABILITY_SUM_TOLERANCE = 0.02;

/** Keys must be exactly the options asked, each a probability, mass summing to 1 (within rounding). */
function distribution(value: unknown, options: readonly string[]): Record<string, number> | null {
  if (!isRecord(value) || Object.keys(value).length !== options.length) return null;
  let mass = 0;
  for (const option of options) { const p = value[option]; if (!probability(p)) return null; mass += p; }
  return Math.abs(mass - 1) <= PROBABILITY_SUM_TOLERANCE ? (value as Record<string, number>) : null;
}

/** Every requested question must come back with the type that was asked and numbers in range; otherwise the whole set is bad. */
export function parseAnswers(json: unknown, questions: QuestionMap):
  | { ok: true; answers: Record<string, Answer>; model: string; inputTokens: number; outputTokens: number }
  | { ok: false; detail: string } {
  if (!isRecord(json) || !isRecord(json.answers)) return { ok: false, detail: "no answers object" };
  const answers: Record<string, Answer> = {};
  for (const [id, q] of Object.entries(questions)) {
    const parsed = parseOne(json.answers[id], q);
    if (typeof parsed === "string") return { ok: false, detail: `answer for "${id}": ${parsed}` };
    answers[id] = parsed;
  }
  const usage = isRecord(json.usage) ? json.usage : {};
  return { ok: true, answers, model: typeof json.model === "string" ? json.model : "unknown",
    inputTokens: typeof usage.input_tokens === "number" ? usage.input_tokens : 0, outputTokens: typeof usage.output_tokens === "number" ? usage.output_tokens : 0 };
}

function parseOne(a: unknown, q: Question): Answer | string {
  if (!isRecord(a)) return "missing";
  if (a.type !== q.type) return `is a ${String(a.type)}, and a ${q.type} was asked`;
  if (q.type === "noul") return probability(a.noul) ? { type: "noul", noul: a.noul } : "noul is not a probability";
  const confidence = a.confidence === undefined || a.confidence === null ? 0 : a.confidence;
  if (!probability(confidence)) return "confidence is not a probability";
  const options = Object.keys(q.criteria);
  const probabilities = distribution(a.probabilities, options);
  if (!probabilities) return `probabilities do not cover ${options.length} options, or do not sum to 1`;
  if (typeof a.choice !== "string" || !(a.choice in q.criteria)) return "choice is not one of the options";
  return { type: "choice", choice: a.choice, probabilities, confidence };
}

export class DisabledJudgmentClient implements JudgmentClient {
  readonly enabled = false; readonly model = "disabled";
  async ask<Q extends QuestionMap>(): Promise<JudgmentResult<Q>> { return { ok: false, reason: "disabled", detail: "TYPESAFE_API_KEY is not set", usage: NO_USAGE, ms: 0 }; }
}

export function judgmentClientFromEnv(env: Record<string, string | undefined> = process.env, overrides: Partial<HttpJudgmentConfig> = {}): JudgmentClient {
  const apiKey = env.TYPESAFE_API_KEY?.trim();
  if (!apiKey) return new DisabledJudgmentClient();
  return new HttpJudgmentClient({ apiKey, ...(env.TYPESAFE_BASE_URL ? { baseUrl: env.TYPESAFE_BASE_URL } : {}), ...(env.TYPESAFE_MODEL ? { model: env.TYPESAFE_MODEL } : {}), ...overrides });
}

/** The k most probable options, descending. */
export function topK(probabilities: Record<string, number>, k: number): { value: string; p: number }[] {
  return Object.entries(probabilities).sort(([, a], [, b]) => b - a).slice(0, k).map(([value, p]) => ({ value, p }));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Question helpers and fake**

`packages/judgment/src/questions.ts`:

```ts
import type { ChoiceQuestion, Instructions, JsonValue, NoulQuestion } from "./types";

export function noul(instructions: Instructions, criteria?: { true: string; false: string }): NoulQuestion {
  return { type: "noul", instructions, ...(criteria ? { criteria } : {}) };
}

/** One of a closed list. Include a no-match option unless the options cover every case. */
export function choice<O extends string>(instructions: Instructions, criteria: Record<O, string | { [key: string]: JsonValue } | null>): ChoiceQuestion<O> {
  const n = Object.keys(criteria).length;
  if (n < 2 || n > 255) throw new Error(`a choice takes 2 to 255 options, got ${n}`);
  return { type: "choice", instructions, criteria };
}
```

`packages/judgment/src/fake.ts`:

```ts
import { costUsd, estimateTokens } from "./client";
import type { Answer, AnswersFor, FailureReason, JudgmentClient, JudgmentRequest, JudgmentResult, Question, QuestionMap } from "./types";

export type Scripted = { noul: number } | { choice: string; confidence?: number };
export type Script = Record<string, Scripted> | ((id: string, question: Question, req: JudgmentRequest) => Scripted | undefined);

/** Deterministic and offline. Unscripted questions answer "cannot tell" (0.5, or the last option, flat). */
export class FakeJudgmentClient implements JudgmentClient {
  readonly enabled = true; readonly model = "fake";
  readonly requests: JudgmentRequest[] = [];
  private failure: FailureReason | null = null;
  constructor(private script: Script = {}) {}
  failWith(reason: FailureReason | null): void { this.failure = reason; }

  async ask<Q extends QuestionMap>(req: JudgmentRequest<Q>): Promise<JudgmentResult<Q>> {
    this.requests.push(req);
    const inputTokens = estimateTokens(req.state) + estimateTokens(req.questions as never);
    const usage = { inputTokens, outputTokens: 0, requests: 1, costUsd: costUsd(inputTokens) };
    if (this.failure) return { ok: false, reason: this.failure, usage, ms: 0 };
    const answers: Record<string, Answer> = {};
    for (const [id, q] of Object.entries(req.questions)) {
      const s = typeof this.script === "function" ? this.script(id, q, req) : this.script[id];
      answers[id] = answer(q, s);
    }
    return { ok: true, answers: answers as AnswersFor<Q>, usage, model: "fake", ms: 0 };
  }
}

function answer(q: Question, s: Scripted | undefined): Answer {
  if (q.type === "noul") return { type: "noul", noul: s && "noul" in s ? s.noul : 0.5 };
  const options = Object.keys(q.criteria);
  const wanted = s && "choice" in s ? s.choice : options[options.length - 1]!;
  if (!options.includes(wanted)) throw new Error(`fake judgment: "${wanted}" is not an option`);
  const confidence = s && "choice" in s ? (s.confidence ?? 0.9) : 0;
  const n = options.length;
  // confidence = (p_max - 1/n) / (1 - 1/n)  =>  p_max = confidence * (1 - 1/n) + 1/n
  const pMax = confidence * (1 - 1 / n) + 1 / n;
  const rest = n > 1 ? (1 - pMax) / (n - 1) : 0;
  const probabilities = Object.fromEntries(options.map((o) => [o, o === wanted ? pMax : rest]));
  return { type: "choice", choice: wanted, probabilities, confidence };
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { CHART_TYPES, DIMENSIONS, EDGE_KINDS, GRANULARITIES, INTENTS, METRICS } from "@cheiron/contracts";
import { interpretationQuestions, interpretationState, QUESTION_SET } from "./interpretation";

describe("interpretation questions", () => {
  const q = interpretationQuestions();

  it("asks the eleven questions", () => {
    expect(Object.keys(q).sort()).toEqual(["chart", "chart_named", "combination", "dimension", "edge_kind", "granularity", "in_scope", "intent", "metric", "recruiting_only", "secondary_dimension"].sort());
  });

  it("uses exactly the contract enums as options", () => {
    expect(Object.keys(q.intent.criteria)).toEqual([...INTENTS]);
    expect(Object.keys(q.dimension.criteria)).toEqual([...DIMENSIONS]);
    expect(Object.keys(q.secondary_dimension.criteria)).toEqual([...DIMENSIONS]);
    expect(Object.keys(q.chart.criteria)).toEqual([...CHART_TYPES]);
    expect(Object.keys(q.granularity.criteria)).toEqual([...GRANULARITIES]);
    expect(Object.keys(q.metric.criteria)).toEqual([...METRICS]);
    expect(Object.keys(q.edge_kind.criteria)).toEqual([...EDGE_KINDS]);
  });

  it("gives every option a what, and every choice a way out", () => {
    for (const [id, question] of Object.entries(q)) {
      if (question.type !== "choice") continue;
      for (const [option, c] of Object.entries(question.criteria)) {
        expect(c, `${id}.${option}`).not.toBeNull();
        if (typeof c === "object" && c) expect(c, `${id}.${option}`).toHaveProperty("what");
      }
    }
    expect(q.intent.criteria).toHaveProperty("other");
    expect(q.dimension.criteria).toHaveProperty("none");
  });

  it("builds a small state with only the provided fields", () => {
    expect(interpretationState("q", {})).toEqual({ question: "q", provided: {} });
    expect(interpretationState("q", { drugs: ["a"], start_year: 2015 })).toEqual({ question: "q", provided: { drugs: ["a"], start_year: 2015 } });
  });

  it("is versioned", () => {
    expect(QUESTION_SET).toEqual({ id: "query-interpretation", version: 1 });
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the question set**

`packages/judgment/src/interpretation.ts`. Write the criteria exactly as below; this text is the product.

```ts
import type { ChartType, Dimension, EdgeKind, Granularity, Intent, Metric } from "@cheiron/contracts";
import { choice, noul } from "./questions";
import type { AnswersFor, JsonValue } from "./types";

export const QUESTION_SET = { id: "query-interpretation", version: 1 } as const;

export interface ProvidedFields {
  drugs?: string[]; conditions?: string[]; sponsors?: string[]; countries?: string[];
  trial_phase?: string; status?: string; start_year?: number; end_year?: number;
}

/** The state is deliberately tiny: the question, and what the caller already pinned down. */
export function interpretationState(query: string, provided: ProvidedFields): JsonValue {
  const p: Record<string, JsonValue> = {};
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(provided)) if (v !== undefined) p[k] = v as JsonValue;
  return { question: query, provided: p };
}

const DIMENSION_CRITERIA: Record<Dimension, { what: string; examples?: string[] }> = {
  phase: { what: "Trial phase: early phase 1, phase 1, 2, 3, 4, or not applicable.", examples: ["across phases", "by phase", "phase 3 share"] },
  status: { what: "Recruitment status: recruiting, not yet recruiting, completed, terminated, withdrawn, and so on." },
  sponsor_class: { what: "The kind of lead sponsor: industry, NIH, other government, network, academic or other.", examples: ["sponsor categories", "industry vs academic"] },
  sponsor: { what: "The named organisation that leads the trial.", examples: ["which sponsors", "top companies running"] },
  intervention_type: { what: "The kind of intervention: drug, biological, device, procedure, behavioral, radiation, and so on.", examples: ["intervention types"] },
  drug: { what: "The specific drug or intervention being tested.", examples: ["which drugs", "most studied treatments"] },
  condition: { what: "The disease or condition studied.", examples: ["which conditions", "indications"] },
  country: { what: "The country of the trial sites.", examples: ["by country", "which countries"] },
  start_year: { what: "When the trial started: by year, quarter or month. Pick this for any question about change over time.", examples: ["per year", "since 2015", "over time"] },
  enrollment: { what: "How many participants a trial enrolls (its size).", examples: ["enrollment distribution", "trial sizes"] },
  study_type: { what: "Interventional, observational or expanded access." },
  sex: { what: "Which sexes are eligible: all, female, male." },
  age_group: { what: "Child, adult, older adult." },
  none: { what: "The question does not group, split or rank by any attribute." },
};

export function interpretationQuestions() {
  return {
    in_scope: noul(
      "Judge the user's question (question). Can it be answered from a registry of clinical trials, where each trial has a title, conditions, interventions, phases, a recruitment status, a sponsor and collaborators, site locations with countries, start and completion dates, an enrollment count, a study type, and eligible sexes and age groups?",
      {
        true: "Any question about trials, drugs, conditions, sponsors, sites, phases, enrollment, dates, or relationships between them, even a vague one such as 'what is happening in melanoma research'.",
        false: "A question that needs data the registry does not hold (prices, revenue, stock, efficacy results, dosing, side effects, news), or that is not about clinical trials at all ('what is the weather', 'write a poem').",
      },
    ),
    intent: choice<Intent>("What shape of answer is the user asking for? Judge the question (question) by its meaning, not by the words used.", {
      time_trend: { what: "How a number changes over time: per year, per month, since a date, 'over time', 'trend', 'growth'.", examples: ["How has the number of pembrolizumab trials changed per year since 2015?", "How many melanoma trials started each year?"] },
      distribution: { what: "How trials split across the categories of one attribute, such as phase, status, sponsor type or intervention type.", not_for: "A split over time (time_trend), a top-N list over an open set (ranking), or two or more named things side by side (comparison).", examples: ["How are melanoma trials distributed across phases?", "What are the most common intervention types for diabetes trials?"] },
      comparison: { what: "Two or more NAMED things (drugs, conditions, sponsors, countries) measured side by side on the same attribute.", not_for: "One thing split by categories (distribution).", examples: ["Compare phases for trials involving pembrolizumab vs nivolumab.", "Compare sponsor classes across asthma and COPD trials."] },
      ranking: { what: "Which members of an open-ended set have the most trials: top sponsors, most studied conditions, most common drugs.", not_for: "A closed set such as phases or statuses (distribution); countries (geographic).", examples: ["Which sponsors run the most Alzheimer's trials?", "Top 10 conditions studied with semaglutide."] },
      geographic: { what: "Where trials happen: countries, regions, a map, locations.", examples: ["Which countries have the most recruiting trials for lupus?", "Where are CAR-T trials run?"] },
      network: { what: "Relationships or co-occurrence between entities: which sponsors test which drugs, which drugs are combined, which drugs are tested for which conditions, who collaborates with whom.", examples: ["Show a network of sponsors and drugs for melanoma trials.", "Which drugs frequently co-occur in combination studies with pembrolizumab?"] },
      count: { what: "A single number: how many trials match.", not_for: "A number per category or per period.", examples: ["How many phase 3 psoriasis trials are recruiting?"] },
      lookup: { what: "A list of specific trials or their details, rather than an aggregate.", examples: ["List recruiting trials for pediatric asthma in Canada.", "Which trials test tirzepatide in adolescents?"] },
      other: { what: "None of the above, or the question cannot be answered with trial counts, lists or relationships." },
    }),
    dimension: choice<Dimension>("Which attribute of the trials should the answer be grouped, split or ranked by? Pick 'none' when the question names no attribute. For any question about change over time pick 'start_year'.", DIMENSION_CRITERIA),
    secondary_dimension: choice<Dimension>("Besides the main attribute, does the question ask to split each group further by a SECOND attribute (for example per year AND by phase, or by country AND by sponsor type)? Pick 'none' when there is only one attribute.", DIMENSION_CRITERIA),
    chart: choice<ChartType>("Which visual best answers the question? If the user names a visual form, pick that one.", {
      bar: { what: "One value per category, categories side by side.", not_for: "Change over many periods (line)." },
      grouped_bar: { what: "Several named things compared across the same categories, bars side by side within each category." },
      stacked_bar: { what: "One bar per category subdivided by a second attribute, when the total of each bar matters." },
      line: { what: "A value over time, one line per series." },
      histogram: { what: "How a numeric attribute (enrollment size, start year) is distributed across bins." },
      scatter: { what: "One point per trial on two numeric axes, such as enrollment against start date.", not_for: "Counts per category." },
      network_graph: { what: "Nodes and links showing which entities co-occur or relate." },
      map: { what: "Values per country drawn on a world map." },
      table: { what: "A list of individual trials with their details." },
      single_value: { what: "One number, such as a total count." },
    }),
    chart_named: noul("Does the user explicitly name the kind of visual they want?", {
      true: "The question contains a word for a visual form: chart, graph, plot, map, network, table, list, timeline, histogram, scatter, pie.",
      false: "The question asks for information and leaves the visual form open ('how are', 'which', 'how many', 'compare', 'show me' with no form named).",
    }),
    granularity: choice<Granularity>("For a question about change over time, what period should each point cover?", {
      year: { what: "Per year, or no period named (the default)." },
      quarter: { what: "Per quarter." },
      month: { what: "Per month." },
    }),
    metric: choice<Metric>("What is being counted or measured?", {
      trial_count: { what: "The number of trials (the default when nothing else is named)." },
      enrollment_total: { what: "The total number of participants enrolled across the trials.", examples: ["how many patients", "total enrollment"] },
      enrollment_median: { what: "The typical (median) enrollment per trial.", examples: ["typical trial size", "median enrollment"] },
    }),
    edge_kind: choice<EdgeKind>("For a question about relationships, which two kinds of entities are linked?", {
      sponsor_drug: { what: "Sponsors linked to the drugs they test." },
      drug_drug: { what: "Drugs linked when they are tested together in the same trial (combinations)." },
      condition_drug: { what: "Conditions linked to the drugs tested for them." },
      sponsor_condition: { what: "Sponsors linked to the conditions they study." },
      sponsor_collaborator: { what: "Lead sponsors linked to their collaborators." },
    }),
    recruiting_only: noul("Does the user restrict the answer to trials that are currently recruiting, open, enrolling or ongoing?", {
      true: "Words such as recruiting, open, enrolling, currently running, active, ongoing.",
      false: "No restriction on status, or a different status (completed, terminated) is named.",
    }),
    combination: noul("When the question names more than one drug, does the user mean trials in which those drugs are used TOGETHER (a combination)?", {
      true: "'pembrolizumab plus chemotherapy', 'combined with', 'in combination with', 'together with'.",
      false: "The drugs are alternatives or are being compared: 'or', 'versus', 'vs', 'compare', 'either'. Also when only one drug is named.",
    }),
  };
}

export type InterpretationQuestions = ReturnType<typeof interpretationQuestions>;
export type InterpretationAnswers = AnswersFor<InterpretationQuestions>;
```

```ts
      study_type: "INTERVENTIONAL", sex: "ALL", std_ages: ["ADULT", "OLDER_ADULT"],
    });
  });

  });
});

    expect(page).toMatchObject({ totalCount: 5, nextPageToken: "abc" });
    expect(page?.studies).toHaveLength(1);
  });

    expect(normalizePage({ nope: 1 })).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { buildStudiesUrl, essieAdvanced, essieTerms, STUDY_FIELDS } from "./url";

describe("essie", () => {
  it("quotes terms and joins them", () => {
    expect(essieTerms(["pembrolizumab"], "OR")).toBe('"pembrolizumab"');
    expect(essieTerms(["non-small cell lung cancer", "melanoma"], "OR")).toBe('"non-small cell lung cancer" OR "melanoma"');
    expect(essieTerms(["a", "b"], "AND")).toBe('"a" AND "b"');
  });
  it("strips quotes inside a term", () => {
    expect(essieTerms(['say "hi"'], "OR")).toBe('"say hi"');
  });
  it("builds the advanced filter", () => {
    expect(essieAdvanced({})).toBeUndefined();
    expect(essieAdvanced({ phase: "PHASE3" })).toBe("AREA[Phase]PHASE3");
    expect(essieAdvanced({ startYear: 2015 })).toBe("AREA[StartDate]RANGE[2015-01-01,MAX]");
    expect(essieAdvanced({ endYear: 2020 })).toBe("AREA[StartDate]RANGE[MIN,2020-12-31]");
    expect(essieAdvanced({ phase: "PHASE2", startYear: 2015, endYear: 2020 })).toBe("AREA[Phase]PHASE2 AND AREA[StartDate]RANGE[2015-01-01,2020-12-31]");
  });
});

describe("buildStudiesUrl", () => {
  it("has 19 fields", () => expect(STUDY_FIELDS).toHaveLength(19));
  it("encodes every parameter and the field list", () => {
    const url = new URL(buildStudiesUrl({ intr: '"pembrolizumab"', cond: '"melanoma"', overallStatus: "RECRUITING", advanced: "AREA[Phase]PHASE3", pageSize: 1000, countTotal: true, sort: "StartDate:desc" }));
    expect(url.origin + url.pathname).toBe("https://clinicaltrials.gov/api/v2/studies");
    expect(url.searchParams.get("query.intr")).toBe('"pembrolizumab"');
    expect(url.searchParams.get("query.cond")).toBe('"melanoma"');
    expect(url.searchParams.get("filter.overallStatus")).toBe("RECRUITING");
    expect(url.searchParams.get("filter.advanced")).toBe("AREA[Phase]PHASE3");
    expect(url.searchParams.get("fields")).toBe(STUDY_FIELDS.join(","));
    expect(url.searchParams.get("pageSize")).toBe("1000");
    expect(url.searchParams.get("countTotal")).toBe("true");
    expect(url.searchParams.get("sort")).toBe("StartDate:desc");
    expect(url.searchParams.get("format")).toBe("json");
    expect(url.searchParams.has("query.spons")).toBe(false);
  });
  it("carries the page token", () => {
    expect(new URL(buildStudiesUrl({ pageToken: "t" })).searchParams.get("pageToken")).toBe("t");
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Study model**

`packages/ctgov/src/study.ts`:

```ts
/** A registry record, flattened to what the engine reads. Nothing here is inferred: missing is null or empty. */
export interface Study {
  nct_id: string;
  title: string;
  status: string;
  phases: string[];
  start_date: string | null;
  start_year: number | null;
  primary_completion_date: string | null;
  lead_sponsor: { name: string; class: string };
  collaborators: string[];
  conditions: string[];
  mesh_conditions: string[];
  interventions: { name: string; type: string }[];
  mesh_interventions: string[];
  /** Distinct, in first-seen order. */
  countries: string[];
  enrollment: number | null;
  study_type: string;
  sex: string;
  std_ages: string[];
}

type Rec = Record<string, unknown>;
const rec = (v: unknown): Rec => (typeof v === "object" && v !== null && !Array.isArray(v) ? (v as Rec) : {});
const str = (v: unknown, fallback = ""): string => (typeof v === "string" ? v : fallback);
const strOrNull = (v: unknown): string | null => (typeof v === "string" && v ? v : null);
const list = (v: unknown): unknown[] => (Array.isArray(v) ? v : []);
const strings = (v: unknown): string[] => list(v).filter((x): x is string => typeof x === "string");
const names = (v: unknown, key: string): string[] => list(v).map((x) => str(rec(x)[key])).filter(Boolean);

export function normalizeStudy(raw: unknown): Study | null {
  const p = rec(rec(raw).protocolSection);
  const d = rec(rec(raw).derivedSection);
  const nct_id = str(rec(p.identificationModule).nctId);
  if (!/^NCT\d{8}$/.test(nct_id)) return null;
  const status = rec(p.statusModule);
  const start_date = strOrNull(rec(status.startDateStruct).date);
  const sponsor = rec(rec(p.sponsorCollaboratorsModule).leadSponsor);
  const design = rec(p.designModule);
  const count = rec(design.enrollmentInfo).count;
  const countries: string[] = [];
  for (const loc of list(rec(p.contactsLocationsModule).locations)) {
    const c = str(rec(loc).country);
    if (c && !countries.includes(c)) countries.push(c);
  }
  return {
    nct_id,
    title: str(rec(p.identificationModule).briefTitle),
    status: str(status.overallStatus, "UNKNOWN"),
    phases: strings(design.phases),
    start_date,
    start_year: start_date && /^\d{4}/.test(start_date) ? Number(start_date.slice(0, 4)) : null,
    primary_completion_date: strOrNull(rec(status.primaryCompletionDateStruct).date),
    lead_sponsor: { name: str(sponsor.name), class: str(sponsor.class, "UNKNOWN") },
    collaborators: names(rec(p.sponsorCollaboratorsModule).collaborators, "name"),
    conditions: strings(rec(p.conditionsModule).conditions),
    mesh_conditions: names(rec(d.conditionBrowseModule).meshes, "term"),
    interventions: list(rec(p.armsInterventionsModule).interventions).map((i) => ({ name: str(rec(i).name), type: str(rec(i).type, "OTHER") })).filter((i) => i.name),
    mesh_interventions: names(rec(d.interventionBrowseModule).meshes, "term"),
    countries,
    enrollment: typeof count === "number" && Number.isFinite(count) ? count : null,
    study_type: str(design.studyType, "UNKNOWN"),
    sex: str(rec(p.eligibilityModule).sex, "ALL"),
    std_ages: strings(rec(p.eligibilityModule).stdAges),
  };
}

export function normalizePage(json: unknown): { studies: Study[]; totalCount: number | null; nextPageToken: string | null } | null {
  const r = rec(json);
  if (!Array.isArray(r.studies)) return null;
  return {
    studies: r.studies.map(normalizeStudy).filter((s): s is Study => s !== null),
    totalCount: typeof r.totalCount === "number" ? r.totalCount : null,
    nextPageToken: typeof r.nextPageToken === "string" ? r.nextPageToken : null,
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: URL builder**

`packages/ctgov/src/url.ts`:

```ts
export const CTGOV_BASE_URL = "https://clinicaltrials.gov/api/v2";

/** Exactly what the engine reads. Verified valid against the live API on 2026-10-09. */
export const STUDY_FIELDS = [
  "NCTId", "BriefTitle", "OverallStatus", "Phase", "StartDate", "PrimaryCompletionDate",
  "LeadSponsorName", "LeadSponsorClass", "CollaboratorName", "Condition", "InterventionName", "InterventionType",
  "LocationCountry", "EnrollmentCount", "StudyType", "Sex", "StdAge", "ConditionMeshTerm", "InterventionMeshTerm",
] as const;

export interface StudiesQuery {
  intr?: string; cond?: string; spons?: string; locn?: string;
  overallStatus?: string; advanced?: string;
  pageSize?: number; pageToken?: string; countTotal?: boolean; sort?: string;
  fields?: readonly string[];
}

/** Essie: each term quoted (inner quotes removed), joined by the operator. */
export function essieTerms(terms: string[], op: "AND" | "OR"): string {
  return terms.map((t) => `"${t.replace(/"/g, "").trim()}"`).join(` ${op} `);
}

export function essieAdvanced(parts: { phase?: string; startYear?: number; endYear?: number }): string | undefined {
  const clauses: string[] = [];
  if (parts.phase) clauses.push(`AREA[Phase]${parts.phase}`);
  if (parts.startYear !== undefined || parts.endYear !== undefined) {
    const lo = parts.startYear !== undefined ? `${parts.startYear}-01-01` : "MIN";
    const hi = parts.endYear !== undefined ? `${parts.endYear}-12-31` : "MAX";
    clauses.push(`AREA[StartDate]RANGE[${lo},${hi}]`);
  }
  return clauses.length ? clauses.join(" AND ") : undefined;
}

export function buildStudiesUrl(q: StudiesQuery, baseUrl = CTGOV_BASE_URL): string {
  const url = new URL(`${baseUrl.replace(/\/+$/, "")}/studies`);
  const set = (k: string, v: string | number | boolean | undefined) => { if (v !== undefined && v !== "") url.searchParams.set(k, String(v)); };
  set("format", "json");
  set("query.intr", q.intr); set("query.cond", q.cond); set("query.spons", q.spons); set("query.locn", q.locn);
  set("filter.overallStatus", q.overallStatus); set("filter.advanced", q.advanced);
  set("fields", (q.fields ?? STUDY_FIELDS).join(","));
  set("pageSize", q.pageSize); set("pageToken", q.pageToken); set("countTotal", q.countTotal); set("sort", q.sort);
  return url.toString();
}
```

```ts
const FIXTURES: Record<string, Parameters<typeof buildStudiesUrl>[0]> = {

};

mkdirSync(new URL("../fixtures/", import.meta.url), { recursive: true });
for (const [name, q] of Object.entries(FIXTURES)) {

  const res = await fetch(url);
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${name}: ${res.status}`);
  const json = await res.json();

  console.log(`${name}: ${json.studies.length} studies of ${json.totalCount}`);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Cache**

`packages/ctgov/src/cache.ts`:

```ts
/** Insertion-ordered map with a size cap and a TTL; `get` refreshes recency. */
export class LruCache<V> {
  private readonly map = new Map<string, { value: V; at: number }>();
  constructor(private readonly max: number, private readonly ttlMs: number, private readonly now: () => number = Date.now) {}
  get(key: string): V | undefined {
    const hit = this.map.get(key);
    if (!hit) return undefined;
    if (this.now() - hit.at > this.ttlMs) { this.map.delete(key); return undefined; }
    this.map.delete(key); this.map.set(key, hit);
    return hit.value;
  }
  set(key: string, value: V): void {
    this.map.delete(key);
    this.map.set(key, { value, at: this.now() });
    while (this.map.size > this.max) this.map.delete(this.map.keys().next().value as string);
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Client**

`packages/ctgov/src/client.ts`:

```ts
import { LruCache } from "./cache";
import { normalizePage, type Study } from "./study";
import { buildStudiesUrl, CTGOV_BASE_URL, type StudiesQuery } from "./url";

export type FetchQuery = Pick<StudiesQuery, "intr" | "cond" | "spons" | "locn" | "overallStatus" | "advanced">;
export interface FetchOptions { maxStudies: number; signal?: AbortSignal; onPage?: (fetched: number, total: number) => void }
export interface CtgovRequestLog { url: string; total_count: number; fetched: number }
export type CtgovFailureReason = "ctgov_invalid_request" | "ctgov_timeout" | "ctgov_network" | "ctgov_server_error" | "ctgov_bad_response";
export type CtgovResult =
  | { ok: true; studies: Study[]; totalCount: number; fetched: number; truncated: boolean; requests: CtgovRequestLog[]; ms: number }
  | { ok: false; reason: CtgovFailureReason; detail?: string; requests: CtgovRequestLog[]; ms: number };

export interface CtgovClient {
  fetchStudies(q: FetchQuery, opts: FetchOptions): Promise<CtgovResult>;
  version(): Promise<{ apiVersion: string; dataTimestamp: string }>;
}

export interface HttpCtgovConfig {
  baseUrl?: string; fetch?: typeof fetch; timeoutMs?: number; pageSize?: number;
  cache?: LruCache<unknown>; sleep?: (ms: number) => Promise<void>; now?: () => number;
}

type Page = NonNullable<ReturnType<typeof normalizePage>>;
type PageResult = { ok: true; page: Page; url: string } | { ok: false; reason: CtgovFailureReason; detail?: string; url: string };

export class HttpCtgovClient implements CtgovClient {
  private readonly baseUrl: string; private readonly http: typeof fetch; private readonly timeoutMs: number; private readonly pageSize: number;
  private readonly cache: LruCache<unknown>; private readonly sleep: (ms: number) => Promise<void>; private readonly now: () => number;
  private versionCache: { at: number; value: { apiVersion: string; dataTimestamp: string } } | null = null;

  constructor(cfg: HttpCtgovConfig = {}) {
    this.baseUrl = cfg.baseUrl ?? CTGOV_BASE_URL; this.http = cfg.fetch ?? fetch; this.timeoutMs = cfg.timeoutMs ?? 15_000;
    this.pageSize = cfg.pageSize ?? 1000; this.cache = cfg.cache ?? new LruCache<unknown>(200, 60 * 60 * 1000);
    this.sleep = cfg.sleep ?? ((ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms))); this.now = cfg.now ?? Date.now;
  }

  async fetchStudies(q: FetchQuery, opts: FetchOptions): Promise<CtgovResult> {
    const started = this.now();
    const requests: CtgovRequestLog[] = [];
    const studies: Study[] = [];
    let total = 0; let token: string | undefined; let truncated = false;
    for (let pageNo = 0; ; pageNo++) {
      const url = buildStudiesUrl({ ...q, pageSize: this.pageSize, sort: "StartDate:desc", ...(pageNo === 0 ? { countTotal: true } : {}), ...(token ? { pageToken: token } : {}) }, this.baseUrl);
      const got = await this.page(url, opts.signal);
      if (!got.ok) return { ok: false, reason: got.reason, ...(got.detail ? { detail: got.detail } : {}), requests, ms: this.now() - started };
      if (pageNo === 0) total = got.page.totalCount ?? got.page.studies.length;
      const room = opts.maxStudies - studies.length;
      const take = got.page.studies.slice(0, room);
      studies.push(...take);
      requests.push({ url, total_count: total, fetched: take.length });
      opts.onPage?.(studies.length, total);
      const more = got.page.nextPageToken !== null || got.page.studies.length > take.length;
      if (!more) break;
      if (studies.length >= opts.maxStudies) { truncated = true; break; }
      token = got.page.nextPageToken ?? undefined;
      if (!token) break;
    }
    return { ok: true, studies, totalCount: total, fetched: studies.length, truncated, requests, ms: this.now() - started };
  }

  async version(): Promise<{ apiVersion: string; dataTimestamp: string }> {
    if (this.versionCache && this.now() - this.versionCache.at < 10 * 60 * 1000) return this.versionCache.value;
    try {
      const res = await this.http(`${this.baseUrl}/version`, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(this.timeoutMs) });
      const j = (await res.json()) as { apiVersion?: string; dataTimestamp?: string };
      const value = { apiVersion: j.apiVersion ?? "unknown", dataTimestamp: j.dataTimestamp ?? "unknown" };
      this.versionCache = { at: this.now(), value };
      return value;
    } catch {
      return this.versionCache?.value ?? { apiVersion: "unknown", dataTimestamp: "unknown" };
    }
  }

  /** One page, from cache or the network, with one retry on 429/5xx/network. */
  private async page(url: string, outer?: AbortSignal): Promise<PageResult> {
    const cached = this.cache.get(url) as Page | undefined;
    if (cached) return { ok: true, page: cached, url };
    let last: PageResult = { ok: false, reason: "ctgov_network", url };
    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 2; attempt++) {
      const timeout = AbortSignal.timeout(this.timeoutMs);
      const signal = outer ? AbortSignal.any([outer, timeout]) : timeout;
      let res: Response;
      try {
        res = await this.http(url, { signal, headers: { accept: "application/json" } });
      } catch (e) {
        last = { ok: false, reason: timeout.aborted ? "ctgov_timeout" : "ctgov_network", detail: String(e), url };
        if (outer?.aborted) return last;
        await this.sleep(300 * (attempt + 1)); continue;
      }
      if (res.ok) {
        let json: unknown;
        try { json = await res.json(); } catch (e) { return { ok: false, reason: "ctgov_bad_response", detail: String(e), url }; }
        const page = normalizePage(json);
        if (!page) return { ok: false, reason: "ctgov_bad_response", detail: "not a studies page", url };
        this.cache.set(url, page);
        return { ok: true, page, url };
      }
      const detail = (await res.text().catch(() => "")).slice(0, 300);
      if (res.status === 400 || res.status === 404) return { ok: false, reason: "ctgov_invalid_request", detail, url };
      last = { ok: false, reason: "ctgov_server_error", detail: `${res.status} ${detail}`, url };
      await this.sleep(300 * (attempt + 1));
    }
    return last;
  }
}

/** A client that answers from pages handed to it, for engine and API tests. */
export class FakeCtgovClient implements CtgovClient {
  readonly calls: FetchQuery[] = [];
  constructor(private readonly studies: Study[], private readonly total = studies.length) {}
  async fetchStudies(q: FetchQuery, opts: FetchOptions): Promise<CtgovResult> {
    this.calls.push(q);
    const take = this.studies.slice(0, opts.maxStudies);
    opts.onPage?.(take.length, this.total);
    return { ok: true, studies: take, totalCount: this.total, fetched: take.length, truncated: take.length < this.total, requests: [{ url: buildStudiesUrl({ ...q, pageSize: 1000, countTotal: true, sort: "StartDate:desc" }), total_count: this.total, fetched: take.length }], ms: 1 };
  }
  async version() { return { apiVersion: "2.0.5", dataTimestamp: "2026-10-08T09:00:05" }; }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`packages/ctgov/src/countries.ts`:

```ts
import countries from "i18n-iso-countries";
import en from "i18n-iso-countries/langs/en.json" with { type: "json" };

countries.registerLocale(en);

/** The registry's spellings that the library does not resolve by name. */
const OVERRIDES: Record<string, string> = {
  "Korea, Republic of": "KOR", "Korea, Democratic People's Republic of": "PRK", "Russian Federation": "RUS", "Taiwan": "TWN",
  "Türkiye": "TUR", "Turkey": "TUR", "Iran, Islamic Republic of": "IRN", "Viet Nam": "VNM", "Czechia": "CZE", "Czech Republic": "CZE",
  "Moldova, Republic of": "MDA", "Tanzania": "TZA", "Bolivia": "BOL", "Venezuela": "VEN", "Syrian Arab Republic": "SYR",
  "Lao People's Democratic Republic": "LAO", "Macedonia, The Former Yugoslav Republic of": "MKD", "North Macedonia": "MKD",
  "Congo, The Democratic Republic of the": "COD", "Côte D'Ivoire": "CIV", "Hong Kong": "HKG", "Macao": "MAC", "Palestinian Territory, occupied": "PSE",
  "Brunei Darussalam": "BRN", "Libyan Arab Jamahiriya": "LBY", "Micronesia, Federated States of": "FSM", "Holy See (Vatican City State)": "VAT",
  "United States": "USA", "United Kingdom": "GBR",
};

export function iso3(country: string): string | null {
  const name = country.trim();
  const direct = OVERRIDES[name];
  if (direct) return direct;
  const alpha2 = countries.getAlpha2Code(name, "en");
  if (!alpha2) return null;
  return countries.alpha2ToAlpha3(alpha2) ?? null;
}

export function iso3ToNumeric(code: string): string | null {
  return countries.alpha3ToNumeric(code) ?? null;
}
```

If `getAlpha2Code` is not exported under that name in the installed version, use `countries.getSimpleAlpha2Code(name, "en")`; check `node_modules/i18n-iso-countries/index.d.ts`.

### Task 9: Engine dimensions: how a study falls into buckets

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/engine/package.json`, `packages/engine/src/dimensions.ts`, `packages/engine/src/dimensions.test.ts`, `packages/engine/src/test-studies.ts`, `packages/engine/src/index.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:

```ts
export const ENROLLMENT_BINS: { label: string; min: number; max: number | null }[];
export function drugIdentity(s: Study): string[];           // mesh terms, else lowercased DRUG/BIOLOGICAL names, deduped
export function conditionIdentity(s: Study): string[];      // mesh conditions, else conditions, deduped
export function timeKey(s: Study, g: Granularity): string | null;   // "2019" | "2019-Q2" | "2019-05"
export function valuesOf(s: Study, d: Dimension, g?: Granularity | null): string[];  // bucket keys; [] when the study has no value
export function isMultiValued(d: Dimension): boolean;
export function fixedOrder(d: Dimension): string[] | null;
export function bucketLabel(d: Dimension, key: string): string;
export const DIMENSION_LABEL: Record<Dimension, string>;
export const DIMENSION_LABEL_PLURAL: Record<Dimension, string>;
export function citationField(d: Dimension): string;
export function citationExcerpt(s: Study, d: Dimension, key: string): string;
export function studyLabel(s: Study): string;               // title or nct_id
```

```ts
import type { Study } from "@cheiron/ctgov";

export function study(p: Partial<Study> & { nct_id: string }): Study {
  const start_date = p.start_date === undefined ? "2020-01-15" : p.start_date;
  return {
    title: `Study ${p.nct_id}`, status: "COMPLETED", phases: ["PHASE2"], start_date,
    start_year: start_date ? Number(start_date.slice(0, 4)) : null, primary_completion_date: null,
    lead_sponsor: { name: "Acme", class: "INDUSTRY" }, collaborators: [], conditions: ["Melanoma"], mesh_conditions: ["Melanoma"],
    interventions: [{ name: "Pembrolizumab", type: "BIOLOGICAL" }], mesh_interventions: ["pembrolizumab"],
    countries: ["United States"], enrollment: 100, study_type: "INTERVENTIONAL", sex: "ALL", std_ages: ["ADULT"],
    ...p,
  };
}

/** Twelve studies that exercise every rule: multi-valued phases, missing dates, no enrollment, several countries, combinations. */
export const SAMPLE: Study[] = [
  study({ nct_id: "NCT00000001", phases: ["PHASE3"], start_date: "2021-03-01", enrollment: 500, countries: ["United States", "Germany"], lead_sponsor: { name: "Merck", class: "INDUSTRY" }, mesh_interventions: ["pembrolizumab", "lenvatinib"], interventions: [{ name: "Pembrolizumab", type: "BIOLOGICAL" }, { name: "Lenvatinib", type: "DRUG" }] }),
  study({ nct_id: "NCT00000002", phases: ["PHASE1", "PHASE2"], start_date: "2021-07-10", enrollment: 40, lead_sponsor: { name: "Merck", class: "INDUSTRY" } }),
  study({ nct_id: "NCT00000003", phases: ["PHASE2"], start_date: "2020-11-05", enrollment: null, lead_sponsor: { name: "NCI", class: "NIH" }, countries: ["Canada"] }),
  study({ nct_id: "NCT00000004", phases: [], start_date: null, enrollment: 20, status: "RECRUITING", study_type: "OBSERVATIONAL" }),
  study({ nct_id: "NCT00000005", phases: ["PHASE3"], start_date: "2019-05-20", enrollment: 1200, countries: ["Germany", "France"], mesh_conditions: ["Melanoma", "Lung Neoplasms"], mesh_interventions: ["nivolumab"], interventions: [{ name: "Nivolumab", type: "BIOLOGICAL" }], lead_sponsor: { name: "Bristol", class: "INDUSTRY" } }),
  study({ nct_id: "NCT00000006", phases: ["PHASE2"], start_date: "2019-01-01", enrollment: 80, mesh_interventions: [], interventions: [{ name: "Pembrolizumab 200mg", type: "BIOLOGICAL" }, { name: "pembrolizumab", type: "BIOLOGICAL" }, { name: "Placebo", type: "DRUG" }] }),
```

```ts
    expect(fixedOrder("enrollment")).toEqual(["1–50", "51–100", "101–200", "201–500", "501–1000", "1001+"]);
    expect(fixedOrder("sponsor")).toBeNull();
  });
  it("labels phases and classes", () => {
    expect(bucketLabel("phase", "PHASE3")).toBe("Phase 3"); expect(bucketLabel("phase", "NA")).toBe("Not applicable");
    expect(bucketLabel("sponsor_class", "OTHER_GOV")).toBe("Other government"); expect(bucketLabel("country", "Japan")).toBe("Japan");
```
