- [ ] **Step 5: Implement**

`packages/engine/src/dimensions.ts`:

```ts
import type { Dimension, Granularity } from "@cheiron/contracts";
import type { Study } from "@cheiron/ctgov";

export const ENROLLMENT_BINS: { label: string; min: number; max: number | null }[] = [
  { label: "1–50", min: 1, max: 50 }, { label: "51–100", min: 51, max: 100 }, { label: "101–200", min: 101, max: 200 },
  { label: "201–500", min: 201, max: 500 }, { label: "501–1000", min: 501, max: 1000 }, { label: "1001+", min: 1001, max: null },
];

const dedupeCi = (xs: string[]): string[] => {
  const seen = new Set<string>(); const out: string[] = [];
  for (const x of xs) { const k = x.trim().toLowerCase(); if (k && !seen.has(k)) { seen.add(k); out.push(x.trim()); } }
  return out;
};

const DRUG_TYPES = new Set(["DRUG", "BIOLOGICAL"]);

/** MeSH terms when the registry mapped any; else the drug/biological names, lowercased. Deduped case-insensitively. */
export function drugIdentity(s: Study): string[] {
  if (s.mesh_interventions.length) return dedupeCi(s.mesh_interventions.map((t) => t.toLowerCase()));
  return dedupeCi(s.interventions.filter((i) => DRUG_TYPES.has(i.type)).map((i) => i.name.toLowerCase()));
}

export function conditionIdentity(s: Study): string[] {
  return dedupeCi(s.mesh_conditions.length ? s.mesh_conditions : s.conditions);
}

export function timeKey(s: Study, g: Granularity): string | null {
  if (!s.start_date || !/^\d{4}/.test(s.start_date)) return null;
  const year = s.start_date.slice(0, 4);
  if (g === "year") return year;
  const month = /^\d{4}-(\d{2})/.exec(s.start_date)?.[1];
  if (!month) return g === "month" ? null : null;
  if (g === "month") return `${year}-${month}`;
  return `${year}-Q${Math.floor((Number(month) - 1) / 3) + 1}`;
}

const enrollmentBin = (n: number | null): string | null => {
  if (n === null || n < 1) return null;
  const bin = ENROLLMENT_BINS.find((b) => n >= b.min && (b.max === null || n <= b.max));
  return bin ? bin.label : null;
};

/** The bucket keys a study falls in for a dimension. Empty when it has no value. */
export function valuesOf(s: Study, d: Dimension, g: Granularity | null = "year"): string[] {
  switch (d) {
    case "phase": return s.phases;
    case "status": return [s.status];
    case "sponsor_class": return [s.lead_sponsor.class];
    case "sponsor": return s.lead_sponsor.name ? [s.lead_sponsor.name] : [];
    case "intervention_type": return [...new Set(s.interventions.map((i) => i.type))];
    case "drug": return drugIdentity(s);
    case "condition": return conditionIdentity(s);
    case "country": return s.countries;
    case "start_year": { const k = timeKey(s, g ?? "year"); return k ? [k] : []; }
    case "enrollment": { const b = enrollmentBin(s.enrollment); return b ? [b] : []; }
    case "study_type": return [s.study_type];
    case "sex": return [s.sex];
    case "age_group": return s.std_ages;
    case "none": return [];
  }
}

export const isMultiValued = (d: Dimension): boolean => ["phase", "intervention_type", "drug", "condition", "country", "age_group"].includes(d);

const PHASE_ORDER = ["EARLY_PHASE1", "PHASE1", "PHASE2", "PHASE3", "PHASE4", "NA"];
const STATUS_ORDER = ["RECRUITING", "NOT_YET_RECRUITING", "ENROLLING_BY_INVITATION", "ACTIVE_NOT_RECRUITING", "COMPLETED", "SUSPENDED", "TERMINATED", "WITHDRAWN", "AVAILABLE", "NO_LONGER_AVAILABLE", "TEMPORARILY_NOT_AVAILABLE", "APPROVED_FOR_MARKETING", "WITHHELD", "UNKNOWN"];
const CLASS_ORDER = ["INDUSTRY", "NIH", "FED", "OTHER_GOV", "NETWORK", "OTHER", "INDIV", "UNKNOWN", "AMBIG"];
const TYPE_ORDER = ["DRUG", "BIOLOGICAL", "DEVICE", "PROCEDURE", "BEHAVIORAL", "RADIATION", "DIETARY_SUPPLEMENT", "GENETIC", "COMBINATION_PRODUCT", "DIAGNOSTIC_TEST", "OTHER"];

export function fixedOrder(d: Dimension): string[] | null {
  switch (d) {
    case "phase": return PHASE_ORDER;
    case "status": return STATUS_ORDER;
    case "sponsor_class": return CLASS_ORDER;
    case "intervention_type": return TYPE_ORDER;
    case "enrollment": return ENROLLMENT_BINS.map((b) => b.label);
    case "study_type": return ["INTERVENTIONAL", "OBSERVATIONAL", "EXPANDED_ACCESS"];
    case "sex": return ["ALL", "FEMALE", "MALE"];
    case "age_group": return ["CHILD", "ADULT", "OLDER_ADULT"];
    default: return null;
  }
}

const title = (s: string): string => s.toLowerCase().replace(/_/g, " ").replace(/^\w/, (c) => c.toUpperCase());
const PHASE_LABEL: Record<string, string> = { EARLY_PHASE1: "Early Phase 1", PHASE1: "Phase 1", PHASE2: "Phase 2", PHASE3: "Phase 3", PHASE4: "Phase 4", NA: "Not applicable" };
const CLASS_LABEL: Record<string, string> = { INDUSTRY: "Industry", NIH: "NIH", FED: "US federal", OTHER_GOV: "Other government", NETWORK: "Network", OTHER: "Other (academic, hospital)", INDIV: "Individual", UNKNOWN: "Unknown", AMBIG: "Ambiguous" };

export function bucketLabel(d: Dimension, key: string): string {
  switch (d) {
    case "phase": return PHASE_LABEL[key] ?? key;
    case "sponsor_class": return CLASS_LABEL[key] ?? key;
    case "status": case "intervention_type": case "study_type": case "sex": case "age_group": return title(key);
    default: return key;
  }
}

export const DIMENSION_LABEL: Record<Dimension, string> = {
  phase: "Phase", status: "Status", sponsor_class: "Sponsor class", sponsor: "Sponsor", intervention_type: "Intervention type",
  drug: "Drug", condition: "Condition", country: "Country", start_year: "Start date", enrollment: "Enrollment",
  study_type: "Study type", sex: "Sex", age_group: "Age group", none: "",
};
export const DIMENSION_LABEL_PLURAL: Record<Dimension, string> = {
  phase: "phases", status: "statuses", sponsor_class: "sponsor classes", sponsor: "sponsors", intervention_type: "intervention types",
  drug: "drugs", condition: "conditions", country: "countries", start_year: "years", enrollment: "enrollment sizes",
  study_type: "study types", sex: "sexes", age_group: "age groups", none: "",
};

export function citationField(d: Dimension): string {
  switch (d) {
    case "phase": return "protocolSection.designModule.phases";
    case "status": return "protocolSection.statusModule.overallStatus";
    case "sponsor_class": return "protocolSection.sponsorCollaboratorsModule.leadSponsor.class";
    case "sponsor": return "protocolSection.sponsorCollaboratorsModule.leadSponsor.name";
    case "intervention_type": return "protocolSection.armsInterventionsModule.interventions[].type";
    case "drug": return "derivedSection.interventionBrowseModule.meshes[].term | protocolSection.armsInterventionsModule.interventions[].name";
    case "condition": return "derivedSection.conditionBrowseModule.meshes[].term | protocolSection.conditionsModule.conditions";
    case "country": return "protocolSection.contactsLocationsModule.locations[].country";
    case "start_year": return "protocolSection.statusModule.startDateStruct.date";
    case "enrollment": return "protocolSection.designModule.enrollmentInfo.count";
    case "study_type": return "protocolSection.designModule.studyType";
    case "sex": return "protocolSection.eligibilityModule.sex";
    case "age_group": return "protocolSection.eligibilityModule.stdAges";
    case "none": return "protocolSection.identificationModule.nctId";
  }
}

/** The exact registry value that put the study in the bucket `key`. */
export function citationExcerpt(s: Study, d: Dimension, key: string): string {
  switch (d) {
    case "start_year": return s.start_date ?? "";
    case "enrollment": return s.enrollment === null ? "" : String(s.enrollment);
    case "drug": {
      const mesh = s.mesh_interventions.find((t) => t.toLowerCase() === key.toLowerCase());
      return mesh ?? s.interventions.find((i) => i.name.toLowerCase() === key.toLowerCase())?.name ?? key;
    }
    case "condition": return s.mesh_conditions.find((t) => t.toLowerCase() === key.toLowerCase()) ?? s.conditions.find((c) => c.toLowerCase() === key.toLowerCase()) ?? key;
    case "none": return s.nct_id;
    default: return key;
  }
}

export const studyLabel = (s: Study): string => s.title || s.nct_id;
```

### Task 10: The planner: thresholds, arms, and the chart compatibility matrix

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/engine/src/plan.ts`, `packages/engine/src/plan.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/engine/src/index.ts` (add `export * from "./plan";`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `InterpretationAnswers`, `QUESTION_SET`, `topK` from `@cheiron/judgment`; `essieTerms`, `essieAdvanced`, `FetchQuery` from `@cheiron/ctgov`; `QueryRequest`, `asList`, `Interpretation` from `@cheiron/contracts`.
- Produces:

```ts
export interface Entities { drugs: string[]; conditions: string[]; sponsors: string[]; countries: string[]; start_year: number | null; end_year: number | null; limit: number | null }
export interface Arm { label: string; query: FetchQuery }
export interface Plan {
  intent: Intent; chart: ChartType; dimension: Dimension | null; secondary: Dimension | null;
  metric: Metric; granularity: Granularity | null; edge_kind: EdgeKind | null;
  arms: Arm[]; limit: number | null; entities: Entities; recruiting_only: boolean; notes: string[];
  interpretation: Interpretation;
}
export type PlanResult = { ok: true; plan: Plan } | { ok: false; reason: "not_answerable"; message: string; interpretation: Interpretation };
export interface PlanInput { answers: InterpretationAnswers; entities: Entities; request: QueryRequest; models: { judge: string; extractor: string; titler: string } }
export const THRESHOLDS: { in_scope: 0.5; intent_alternatives: 0.4; chart_named: 0.6; recruiting_only: 0.7; combination: 0.6 };
export const DEFAULT_LIMITS: { ranking: 10; country_bar: 15; table: 25; network: 40; open_distribution: 10 };
export function defaultChart(intent: Intent, dimension: Dimension | null, secondary: Dimension | null): ChartType;
export function allowedCharts(intent: Intent, dimension: Dimension | null, secondary: Dimension | null, explicit: boolean): ChartType[];
export function plan(input: PlanInput): PlanResult;
export function armFilters(arm: Arm): Record<string, string>;   // { "query.intr": "...", "filter.overallStatus": "..." } for meta.arms
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`packages/engine/src/plan.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { QueryRequest } from "@cheiron/contracts";
import { FakeJudgmentClient, interpretationQuestions, type InterpretationAnswers, type Scripted } from "@cheiron/judgment";
import { allowedCharts, defaultChart, plan, type Entities } from "./plan";

const models = { judge: "fake", extractor: "fake", titler: "fake" };
const none: Entities = { drugs: [], conditions: [], sponsors: [], countries: [], start_year: null, end_year: null, limit: null };

async function answers(script: Record<string, Scripted>): Promise<InterpretationAnswers> {
  const base: Record<string, Scripted> = { in_scope: { noul: 0.95 }, intent: { choice: "distribution" }, dimension: { choice: "phase" }, secondary_dimension: { choice: "none" }, chart: { choice: "bar" }, chart_named: { noul: 0.1 }, granularity: { choice: "year" }, metric: { choice: "trial_count" }, edge_kind: { choice: "sponsor_drug" }, recruiting_only: { noul: 0.1 }, combination: { noul: 0.1 } };
  const r = await new FakeJudgmentClient({ ...base, ...script }).ask({ state: "s", questions: interpretationQuestions() });
  if (!r.ok) throw new Error("fake failed");
  return r.answers;
}
const req = (extra: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => QueryRequest.parse({ query: "q", ...extra });
const okPlan = (r: ReturnType<typeof plan>) => { if (!r.ok) throw new Error(r.message); return r.plan; };

describe("plan: scope and intent", () => {
  it("refuses out-of-scope questions", async () => {
    const r = plan({ answers: await answers({ in_scope: { noul: 0.2 } }), entities: none, request: req(), models });
    expect(r).toMatchObject({ ok: false, reason: "not_answerable" });
  });
  it("refuses intent other", async () => {
    expect(plan({ answers: await answers({ intent: { choice: "other" } }), entities: none, request: req(), models }).ok).toBe(false);
  });
  it("a request intent overrides Jev and skips the scope check", async () => {
    const p = okPlan(plan({ answers: await answers({ in_scope: { noul: 0.2 }, intent: { choice: "other" } }), entities: none, request: req({ intent: "count" }), models }));
    expect(p.intent).toBe("count"); expect(p.chart).toBe("single_value"); expect(p.interpretation.intent.p).toBe(1);
  });
  it("keeps alternatives only when the intent is uncertain", async () => {
    const sure = okPlan(plan({ answers: await answers({ intent: { choice: "ranking", confidence: 0.9 } }), entities: none, request: req(), models }));
    expect(sure.interpretation.intent.alternatives).toEqual([]);
    const unsure = okPlan(plan({ answers: await answers({ intent: { choice: "ranking", confidence: 0.2 } }), entities: none, request: req(), models }));
    expect(unsure.interpretation.intent.alternatives).toHaveLength(3);
    expect(unsure.interpretation.intent.alternatives[0]).toMatchObject({ value: "ranking" });
  });
});

describe("plan: dimensions", () => {
  it("time trends always run over start_year with the granularity", async () => {
    const p = okPlan(plan({ answers: await answers({ intent: { choice: "time_trend" }, dimension: { choice: "phase" }, granularity: { choice: "quarter" }, chart: { choice: "line" } }), entities: none, request: req(), models }));
    expect(p.dimension).toBe("start_year"); expect(p.secondary).toBe("phase"); expect(p.granularity).toBe("quarter"); expect(p.chart).toBe("line");
    expect(okPlan(plan({ answers: await answers({ intent: { choice: "time_trend" }, chart: { choice: "bar" } }), entities: none, request: req(), models })).chart).toBe("bar");
  });
  it("a distribution over start_year becomes a time trend", async () => {
    const p = okPlan(plan({ answers: await answers({ dimension: { choice: "start_year" } }), entities: none, request: req(), models }));
    expect(p.intent).toBe("time_trend"); expect(p.notes.join(" ")).toMatch(/time trend/);
  });
  it("a distribution with no dimension falls back to phase with a note", async () => {
    const p = okPlan(plan({ answers: await answers({ dimension: { choice: "none" } }), entities: none, request: req(), models }));
    expect(p.dimension).toBe("phase"); expect(p.notes.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });
  it("geographic runs over country; network and count have no dimension", async () => {
    expect(okPlan(plan({ answers: await answers({ intent: { choice: "geographic" }, dimension: { choice: "none" } }), entities: none, request: req(), models })).dimension).toBe("country");
    const n = okPlan(plan({ answers: await answers({ intent: { choice: "network" }, edge_kind: { choice: "drug_drug" } }), entities: none, request: req(), models }));
    expect(n.dimension).toBeNull(); expect(n.edge_kind).toBe("drug_drug"); expect(n.limit).toBe(40);
  });
  it("drops a secondary equal to the primary", async () => {
    const p = okPlan(plan({ answers: await answers({ secondary_dimension: { choice: "phase" } }), entities: none, request: req(), models }));
    expect(p.secondary).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("plan: arms and filters", () => {
  it("builds one arm with no query parameters when nothing is named", async () => {
    const p = okPlan(plan({ answers: await answers({}), entities: none, request: req(), models }));
    expect(p.arms).toEqual([{ label: "All trials", query: {} }]);
  });
  it("joins several drugs with OR, or AND for a combination", async () => {
    const e = { ...none, drugs: ["pembrolizumab", "chemotherapy"] };
    expect(okPlan(plan({ answers: await answers({}), entities: e, request: req(), models })).arms[0]!.query.intr).toBe('"pembrolizumab" OR "chemotherapy"');
    const c = okPlan(plan({ answers: await answers({ combination: { noul: 0.8 } }), entities: e, request: req(), models }));
    expect(c.arms[0]!.query.intr).toBe('"pembrolizumab" AND "chemotherapy"'); expect(c.interpretation.combination).toBe(true);
  });
  it("a comparison makes one arm per compared drug, carrying the common filters", async () => {
    const e = { ...none, drugs: ["pembrolizumab", "nivolumab"], conditions: ["melanoma"], start_year: 2015 };
    const p = okPlan(plan({ answers: await answers({ intent: { choice: "comparison" }, recruiting_only: { noul: 0.9 } }), entities: e, request: req(), models }));
    expect(p.arms.map((a) => a.label)).toEqual(["pembrolizumab", "nivolumab"]);
    expect(p.arms[0]!.query).toEqual({ intr: '"pembrolizumab"', cond: '"melanoma"', overallStatus: "RECRUITING", advanced: "AREA[StartDate]RANGE[2015-01-01,MAX]" });
    expect(p.chart).toBe("grouped_bar");
  });
  it("a comparison with one entity becomes a distribution with a note", async () => {
    const p = okPlan(plan({ answers: await answers({ intent: { choice: "comparison" } }), entities: { ...none, drugs: ["a"] }, request: req(), models }));
    expect(p.intent).toBe("distribution"); expect(p.arms).toHaveLength(1);
  });
  it("request fields become filters", async () => {
    const p = okPlan(plan({ answers: await answers({}), entities: none, request: req({ trial_phase: "PHASE3", status: "COMPLETED", end_year: 2020 }), models }));
    expect(p.arms[0]!.query).toEqual({ overallStatus: "COMPLETED", advanced: "AREA[Phase]PHASE3 AND AREA[StartDate]RANGE[MIN,2020-12-31]" });
    expect(p.interpretation.provided_fields).toEqual(["trial_phase", "status", "end_year"]);
  });
  it("limits default by intent and follow the extracted number", async () => {
    expect(okPlan(plan({ answers: await answers({ intent: { choice: "ranking" }, dimension: { choice: "sponsor" } }), entities: none, request: req(), models })).limit).toBe(10);
    expect(okPlan(plan({ answers: await answers({ intent: { choice: "ranking" }, dimension: { choice: "sponsor" } }), entities: { ...none, limit: 5 }, request: req(), models })).limit).toBe(5);
    expect(okPlan(plan({ answers: await answers({ intent: { choice: "geographic" }, chart: { choice: "map" } }), entities: none, request: req(), models })).limit).toBeNull();
    expect(okPlan(plan({ answers: await answers({ intent: { choice: "geographic" }, chart: { choice: "bar" } }), entities: none, request: req(), models })).limit).toBe(15);
  });
});

describe("chart matrix", () => {
  it.each<[Parameters<typeof defaultChart>[0], Parameters<typeof defaultChart>[1], Parameters<typeof defaultChart>[2], string]>([
    ["time_trend", "start_year", null, "line"], ["time_trend", "start_year", "phase", "line"],
    ["distribution", "phase", null, "bar"], ["distribution", "phase", "sponsor_class", "stacked_bar"], ["distribution", "enrollment", null, "histogram"],
    ["comparison", "phase", null, "grouped_bar"], ["comparison", "start_year", null, "line"],
    ["ranking", "sponsor", null, "bar"], ["geographic", "country", null, "map"], ["network", null, null, "network_graph"],
    ["count", null, null, "single_value"], ["lookup", null, null, "table"],
  ])("%s over %s by %s defaults to %s", (intent, dim, sec, chart) => expect(defaultChart(intent, dim, sec)).toBe(chart));

  it("allows only what the shape can render", () => {
    expect(allowedCharts("distribution", "phase", null, false)).toEqual(["bar"]);
    expect(allowedCharts("distribution", "phase", "status", false).sort()).toEqual(["bar", "grouped_bar", "stacked_bar"]);
    expect(allowedCharts("distribution", "enrollment", null, false).sort()).toEqual(["bar", "histogram"]);
    expect(allowedCharts("time_trend", "start_year", null, false).sort()).toEqual(["bar", "line"]);
    expect(allowedCharts("time_trend", "start_year", null, true)).toContain("scatter");
    expect(allowedCharts("ranking", "country", null, false).sort()).toEqual(["bar", "map"]);
    expect(allowedCharts("geographic", "country", null, false).sort()).toEqual(["bar", "map"]);
    expect(allowedCharts("network", null, null, true)).toEqual(["network_graph"]);
  });

  it("applies Jev's chart when compatible and falls back with a reason otherwise", async () => {
    const ok = okPlan(plan({ answers: await answers({ intent: { choice: "geographic" }, chart: { choice: "bar" } }), entities: none, request: req(), models }));
    expect(ok.chart).toBe("bar"); expect(ok.interpretation.chart).toEqual({ chosen: "bar", applied: "bar", explicit: false, reason: null });
    const bad = okPlan(plan({ answers: await answers({ chart: { choice: "line" } }), entities: none, request: req(), models }));
    expect(bad.chart).toBe("bar"); expect(bad.interpretation.chart.chosen).toBe("line"); expect(bad.interpretation.chart.reason).toMatch(/line/);
  });
  it("scatter needs an explicit ask", async () => {
    expect(okPlan(plan({ answers: await answers({ chart: { choice: "scatter" }, chart_named: { noul: 0.2 } }), entities: none, request: req(), models })).chart).toBe("bar");
    const named = okPlan(plan({ answers: await answers({ chart: { choice: "scatter" }, chart_named: { noul: 0.9 } }), entities: none, request: req(), models }));
    expect(named.chart).toBe("scatter"); expect(named.interpretation.chart.explicit).toBe(true);
  });
  it("a request chart counts as explicit", async () => {
    expect(okPlan(plan({ answers: await answers({}), entities: none, request: req({ chart: "scatter" }), models })).chart).toBe("scatter");
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`packages/engine/src/plan.ts`:

```ts
import type { ChartType, Dimension, EdgeKind, Granularity, Intent, Interpretation, Metric, QueryRequest } from "@cheiron/contracts";
import { essieAdvanced, essieTerms, type FetchQuery } from "@cheiron/ctgov";
import { asList } from "@cheiron/contracts";
import { QUESTION_SET, topK, type InterpretationAnswers } from "@cheiron/judgment";
import { DIMENSION_LABEL } from "./dimensions";

export interface Entities { drugs: string[]; conditions: string[]; sponsors: string[]; countries: string[]; start_year: number | null; end_year: number | null; limit: number | null }
export interface Arm { label: string; query: FetchQuery }
export interface Plan {
  intent: Intent; chart: ChartType; dimension: Dimension | null; secondary: Dimension | null;
  metric: Metric; granularity: Granularity | null; edge_kind: EdgeKind | null;
  arms: Arm[]; limit: number | null; entities: Entities; recruiting_only: boolean; notes: string[];
  interpretation: Interpretation;
}
export type PlanResult = { ok: true; plan: Plan } | { ok: false; reason: "not_answerable"; message: string; interpretation: Interpretation };
export interface PlanInput { answers: InterpretationAnswers; entities: Entities; request: QueryRequest; models: { judge: string; extractor: string; titler: string } }

export const THRESHOLDS = { in_scope: 0.5, intent_alternatives: 0.4, chart_named: 0.6, recruiting_only: 0.7, combination: 0.6 } as const;
export const DEFAULT_LIMITS = { ranking: 10, country_bar: 15, table: 25, network: 40, open_distribution: 10 } as const;
const OPEN_DIMENSIONS: Dimension[] = ["sponsor", "drug", "condition", "country"];

export function defaultChart(intent: Intent, dimension: Dimension | null, secondary: Dimension | null): ChartType {
  switch (intent) {
    case "time_trend": return "line";
    case "distribution":
      if (dimension === "enrollment") return "histogram";
      if (dimension === "start_year") return "line";
      return secondary ? "stacked_bar" : "bar";
    case "comparison": return dimension === "start_year" ? "line" : "grouped_bar";
    case "ranking": return "bar";
    case "geographic": return "map";
    case "network": return "network_graph";
    case "count": return "single_value";
    case "lookup": return "table";
    case "other": return "table";
  }
}

/** The matrix from the spec: what the data shape of an intent can render. */
export function allowedCharts(intent: Intent, dimension: Dimension | null, secondary: Dimension | null, explicit: boolean): ChartType[] {
  const set = new Set<ChartType>([defaultChart(intent, dimension, secondary)]);
  switch (intent) {
    case "time_trend":
      set.add("bar"); if (secondary) set.add("stacked_bar"); if (explicit) set.add("scatter"); break;
    case "distribution":
      set.add("bar");
      if (dimension === "enrollment" || dimension === "start_year") set.add("histogram");
      if (dimension === "start_year") set.add("line");
      if (secondary) { set.add("stacked_bar"); set.add("grouped_bar"); }
      if (explicit) set.add("scatter"); break;
    case "comparison":
      set.add("grouped_bar"); set.add("stacked_bar"); if (dimension === "start_year") set.add("line"); if (explicit) set.add("scatter"); break;
    case "ranking":
      set.add("bar"); if (dimension === "country") set.add("map"); break;
    case "geographic":
      set.add("map"); set.add("bar"); break;
    default: break;
  }
  return [...set];
}

const cap = (s: string) => s.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + s.slice(1);

export function plan(input: PlanInput): PlanResult {
  const { answers: a, request, models } = input;
  const notes: string[] = [];
  // Request fields are decisions already taken: they replace what was extracted, field by field.
  const provided_fields = (["drug_name", "condition", "sponsor", "country", "trial_phase", "status", "start_year", "end_year", "intent", "chart"] as const).filter((k) => request[k] !== undefined) as string[];
  const entities: Entities = {
    ...input.entities,
    ...(request.drug_name !== undefined ? { drugs: asList(request.drug_name) } : {}),
    ...(request.condition !== undefined ? { conditions: asList(request.condition) } : {}),
    ...(request.sponsor !== undefined ? { sponsors: asList(request.sponsor) } : {}),
    ...(request.country !== undefined ? { countries: asList(request.country) } : {}),
    ...(request.start_year !== undefined ? { start_year: request.start_year } : {}),
    ...(request.end_year !== undefined ? { end_year: request.end_year } : {}),
  };

  // 1. Scope and intent. A caller-supplied intent is a decision already taken.
  let intent: Intent = request.intent ?? a.intent.choice;
  const intentP = request.intent ? 1 : (a.intent.probabilities[intent] ?? 0);
  const intentConfidence = request.intent ? 1 : a.intent.confidence;

  // 2. Dimensions, normalized by intent.
  let dimension: Dimension | null = a.dimension.choice === "none" ? null : a.dimension.choice;
  let secondary: Dimension | null = a.secondary_dimension.choice === "none" ? null : a.secondary_dimension.choice;
  if (intent === "distribution" && dimension === "start_year") { intent = "time_trend"; notes.push("A split by start year is a time trend; shown as one."); }
  if (intent === "time_trend") { if (dimension && dimension !== "start_year" && !secondary) secondary = dimension; dimension = "start_year"; }
  if (intent === "geographic") dimension = "country";
  if (intent === "network" || intent === "count" || intent === "lookup") { dimension = null; secondary = null; }
  if ((intent === "distribution" || intent === "ranking" || intent === "comparison") && !dimension) { dimension = "phase"; notes.push("No attribute was named; grouped by phase."); }
  if (secondary === "start_year" && intent !== "time_trend") secondary = null;
  if (secondary === dimension) secondary = null;

  const metric: Metric = intent === "network" || intent === "lookup" || intent === "count" ? "trial_count" : a.metric.choice;
  const granularity: Granularity | null = dimension === "start_year" ? a.granularity.choice : null;
  const edge_kind: EdgeKind | null = intent === "network" ? a.edge_kind.choice : null;
  const recruiting_only = request.status === "RECRUITING" || (request.status === undefined && a.recruiting_only.noul >= THRESHOLDS.recruiting_only);
  const combination: boolean | null = entities.drugs.length >= 2 && intent !== "comparison" ? a.combination.noul >= THRESHOLDS.combination : null;

  // 3. Arms.
  const common = (over: Partial<Entities>): FetchQuery => {
    const e = { ...entities, ...over };
    const q: FetchQuery = {};
    if (e.drugs.length) q.intr = essieTerms(e.drugs, combination ? "AND" : "OR");
    if (e.conditions.length) q.cond = essieTerms(e.conditions, "OR");
    if (e.sponsors.length) q.spons = essieTerms(e.sponsors, "OR");
    if (e.countries.length) q.locn = essieTerms(e.countries, "OR");
    const status = request.status ?? (recruiting_only ? "RECRUITING" : undefined);
    if (status) q.overallStatus = status;
    const advanced = essieAdvanced({ ...(request.trial_phase ? { phase: request.trial_phase } : {}), ...(e.start_year !== null ? { startYear: e.start_year } : {}), ...(e.end_year !== null ? { endYear: e.end_year } : {}) });
    if (advanced) q.advanced = advanced;
    return q;
  };
  let arms: Arm[];
  if (intent === "comparison") {
    const axis = (["drugs", "conditions", "sponsors", "countries"] as const).find((k) => entities[k].length >= 2);
    if (!axis) { intent = "distribution"; notes.push("A comparison needs two named things; one was found, so this is shown as a distribution."); arms = [{ label: entityLabel(entities), query: common({}) }]; }
    else arms = entities[axis].map((member) => ({ label: member, query: common({ [axis]: [member] }) }));
  } else {
    arms = [{ label: entityLabel(entities), query: common({}) }];
  }

  // 4. Limit.
  let limit: number | null = entities.limit;
  if (limit === null) {
    if (intent === "ranking") limit = dimension === "country" ? DEFAULT_LIMITS.country_bar : DEFAULT_LIMITS.ranking;
    else if (intent === "network") limit = DEFAULT_LIMITS.network;
    else if (intent === "lookup") limit = DEFAULT_LIMITS.table;
    else if ((intent === "distribution" || intent === "comparison") && dimension && OPEN_DIMENSIONS.includes(dimension)) limit = DEFAULT_LIMITS.open_distribution;
  }

  // 5. Chart: Jev's choice, validated against the shape.
  const chosen: ChartType = request.chart ?? a.chart.choice;
  const explicit = request.chart !== undefined || a.chart_named.noul >= THRESHOLDS.chart_named;
  const allowed = allowedCharts(intent, dimension, secondary, explicit);
  let applied = chosen; let reason: string | null = null;
  if (!allowed.includes(chosen)) {
    applied = defaultChart(intent, dimension, secondary);
    reason = `${chosen} cannot render a ${intent.replace("_", " ")}${dimension ? ` over ${DIMENSION_LABEL[dimension].toLowerCase()}` : ""}; using ${applied}`;
  } else if (chosen === "scatter" && !explicit) {
    applied = defaultChart(intent, dimension, secondary);
    reason = "scatter plots one point per trial and is only used when asked for; using " + applied;
  }
  if (intent === "geographic" && applied === "bar" && limit === null) limit = DEFAULT_LIMITS.country_bar;

  const p = (d: Dimension | null, ans: { probabilities: Record<string, number> }) => (d ? { value: d, p: ans.probabilities[d] ?? 1 } : null);
  const interpretation: Interpretation = {
    intent: { value: intent, p: intentP, confidence: intentConfidence, alternatives: intentConfidence < THRESHOLDS.intent_alternatives ? (topK(a.intent.probabilities, 3) as { value: Intent; p: number }[]) : [] },
    dimension: p(dimension, a.dimension), secondary_dimension: p(secondary, a.secondary_dimension),
    chart: { chosen, applied, explicit, reason },
    metric, granularity, edge_kind,
    entities: { drugs: entities.drugs, conditions: entities.conditions, sponsors: entities.sponsors, countries: entities.countries },
    time_window: { start_year: entities.start_year, end_year: entities.end_year },
    limit: entities.limit, recruiting_only, combination, provided_fields,
    question_set: { id: QUESTION_SET.id, version: QUESTION_SET.version }, models,
  };

  if (!request.intent && a.in_scope.noul < THRESHOLDS.in_scope) {
    return { ok: false, reason: "not_answerable", message: "This does not look like a question the clinical-trials registry can answer.", interpretation: { ...interpretation, chart: { ...interpretation.chart, applied: null } } };
  }
  if (intent === "other") {
    return { ok: false, reason: "not_answerable", message: "No chart answers this question; try asking for a trend, a split, a comparison, a ranking, a map, a network, a count or a list.", interpretation: { ...interpretation, chart: { ...interpretation.chart, applied: null } } };
  }

  return { ok: true, plan: { intent, chart: applied, dimension, secondary, metric, granularity, edge_kind, arms, limit, entities, recruiting_only, notes, interpretation } };
}

/** "Pembrolizumab", "Pembrolizumab · Melanoma", or "All trials". */
export function entityLabel(e: Entities): string {
  const parts = [...e.drugs, ...e.conditions, ...e.sponsors, ...e.countries];
  return parts.length ? parts.map(cap).join(" · ") : "All trials";
}

export function armFilters(arm: Arm): Record<string, string> {
  const out: Record<string, string> = {};
  const q = arm.query;
  if (q.intr) out["query.intr"] = q.intr; if (q.cond) out["query.cond"] = q.cond; if (q.spons) out["query.spons"] = q.spons; if (q.locn) out["query.locn"] = q.locn;
  if (q.overallStatus) out["filter.overallStatus"] = q.overallStatus; if (q.advanced) out["filter.advanced"] = q.advanced;
  return out;
}
```

### Task 11: Buckets and citations

**Files:**

- Modify: `packages/engine/src/index.ts` (add `export * from "./aggregate/buckets"; export * from "./cite";`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `valuesOf, fixedOrder, bucketLabel, isMultiValued, citationField, citationExcerpt, studyLabel` from `./dimensions`.
- Produces:

```ts
export interface Bucket { key: string; label: string; series: string | null; value: number; studies: Study[] }
export interface Bucketized { buckets: Bucket[]; order: string[]; seriesValues: string[]; omitted: number; withoutValue: number; multiValued: boolean }
export interface BucketizeOptions { dimension: Dimension; secondary?: Dimension | null; metric: Metric; granularity?: Granularity | null; limit?: number | null }
export function bucketize(studies: Study[], o: BucketizeOptions): Bucketized;
export function mergeArms(parts: { label: string; result: Bucketized }[], o: BucketizeOptions): Bucketized;   // series = arm label
export function metricValue(studies: Study[], metric: Metric): number;
export function mostRecent(studies: Study[], k: number): Study[];
export function citeBucket(studies: Study[], dimension: Dimension, key: string, k: number): Citation[];
export function citeSelf(s: Study): Citation;
export function citeEdge(studies: Study[], a: { field: string; value: string }, b: { field: string; value: string }, k: number): Citation[];
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`packages/engine/src/aggregate/buckets.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { bucketize, mergeArms, metricValue } from "./buckets";
import { SAMPLE } from "../test-studies";

const byKey = (r: ReturnType<typeof bucketize>, key: string, series: string | null = null) => r.buckets.find((b) => b.key === key && b.series === series);

describe("bucketize", () => {
  it("counts a study once per phase and orders phases", () => {
    const r = bucketize(SAMPLE, { dimension: "phase", metric: "trial_count" });
    expect(r.order).toEqual(["EARLY_PHASE1", "PHASE1", "PHASE2", "PHASE3", "NA"]);
    expect(byKey(r, "PHASE1")?.value).toBe(2);   // NCT02 (1/2) and NCT07
    expect(byKey(r, "PHASE2")?.value).toBe(4);   // NCT02, NCT03, NCT06, NCT10
    expect(byKey(r, "PHASE3")?.value).toBe(4);
    expect(r.multiValued).toBe(true); expect(r.withoutValue).toBe(1);   // NCT04 has no phase
  });
  it("gap-fills years and trims the ends", () => {
    const r = bucketize(SAMPLE.filter((s) => s.start_year !== 2020 && s.start_year !== 2021), { dimension: "start_year", metric: "trial_count", granularity: "year" });
    expect(r.order).toEqual(["2018", "2019", "2020", "2021", "2022", "2023", "2024"]);
    expect(byKey(r, "2020")?.value).toBe(0); expect(byKey(r, "2020")?.studies).toEqual([]);
    expect(r.withoutValue).toBe(1);
  });
  it("quarters", () => {
    const r = bucketize(SAMPLE.filter((s) => s.start_year === 2019), { dimension: "start_year", metric: "trial_count", granularity: "quarter" });
    expect(r.order).toEqual(["2019-Q1", "2019-Q2"]);
  });
  it("ranks an open dimension and counts the omitted", () => {
    const r = bucketize(SAMPLE, { dimension: "sponsor", metric: "trial_count", limit: 2 });
    expect(r.order).toEqual(["Acme", "Merck"]); expect(r.omitted).toBe(3);   // Acme 5, Merck 4
  });
  it("splits by a secondary dimension with zero-filled cells", () => {
    const r = bucketize(SAMPLE, { dimension: "phase", secondary: "sponsor_class", metric: "trial_count" });
    expect(r.seriesValues).toEqual(["INDUSTRY", "NIH", "OTHER"]);
    expect(byKey(r, "PHASE3", "NIH")?.value).toBe(0);
    expect(byKey(r, "PHASE2", "NIH")?.value).toBe(1);
  });
  it("sums and medians enrollment, skipping studies without it", () => {
    const sum = bucketize(SAMPLE, { dimension: "sponsor_class", metric: "enrollment_total" });
    expect(byKey(sum, "NIH")).toBeUndefined(); expect(sum.withoutValue).toBe(1);
    expect(byKey(sum, "OTHER")?.value).toBe(8);
    expect(metricValue(SAMPLE.filter((s) => s.enrollment !== null), "enrollment_median")).toBe(80);
  });
  it("bins enrollment", () => {
    const r = bucketize(SAMPLE, { dimension: "enrollment", metric: "trial_count" });
    expect(r.order).toEqual(["1–50", "51–100", "201–500", "501–1000", "1001+"]);
  });
});

describe("mergeArms", () => {
  it("labels each arm as a series and unions the keys in order", () => {
    const a = bucketize(SAMPLE.filter((s) => s.lead_sponsor.name === "Merck"), { dimension: "phase", metric: "trial_count" });
    const b = bucketize(SAMPLE.filter((s) => s.lead_sponsor.name === "Acme"), { dimension: "phase", metric: "trial_count" });
    const m = mergeArms([{ label: "Merck", result: a }, { label: "Acme", result: b }], { dimension: "phase", metric: "trial_count" });
    expect(m.seriesValues).toEqual(["Merck", "Acme"]);
    expect(m.order).toEqual(["PHASE1", "PHASE2", "PHASE3", "NA"]);
    expect(m.buckets.find((x) => x.key === "PHASE3" && x.series === "Acme")?.value).toBe(0);
    expect(m.buckets.find((x) => x.key === "PHASE3" && x.series === "Merck")?.value).toBe(3);
  });
});
```

`packages/engine/src/cite.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { citeBucket, citeEdge, citeSelf, mostRecent } from "./cite";
import { SAMPLE } from "./test-studies";

describe("citations", () => {
  it("picks the most recently started, nulls last", () => {
    expect(mostRecent(SAMPLE, 3).map((s) => s.nct_id)).toEqual(["NCT00000012", "NCT00000011", "NCT00000010"]);
    expect(mostRecent(SAMPLE, 20).at(-1)?.nct_id).toBe("NCT00000004");
  });
  it("cites the field and exact value for a bucket", () => {
    const c = citeBucket(SAMPLE.filter((s) => s.phases.includes("PHASE3")), "phase", "PHASE3", 2);
    expect(c).toEqual([
      { nct_id: "NCT00000012", title: "Study NCT00000012", field: "protocolSection.designModule.phases", excerpt: "PHASE3" },
      { nct_id: "NCT00000009", title: "Study NCT00000009", field: "protocolSection.designModule.phases", excerpt: "PHASE3" },
    ]);
  });
  it("cites a study for itself", () => {
    expect(citeSelf(SAMPLE[0]!)).toEqual({ nct_id: "NCT00000001", title: "Study NCT00000001", field: "protocolSection.identificationModule.nctId", excerpt: "NCT00000001" });
  });
  it("cites both ends of an edge", () => {
    const c = citeEdge([SAMPLE[0]!], { field: "protocolSection.sponsorCollaboratorsModule.leadSponsor.name", value: "Merck" }, { field: "derivedSection.interventionBrowseModule.meshes[].term", value: "lenvatinib" }, 5);
    expect(c[0]).toMatchObject({ nct_id: "NCT00000001", excerpt: 'leadSponsor.name: "Merck" · meshes[].term: "lenvatinib"' });
    expect(c[0]!.field).toBe("protocolSection.sponsorCollaboratorsModule.leadSponsor.name | derivedSection.interventionBrowseModule.meshes[].term");
  });
  it("respects k = 0", () => expect(citeBucket(SAMPLE, "phase", "PHASE2", 0)).toEqual([]));
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Citations**

`packages/engine/src/cite.ts`:

```ts
import type { Citation, Dimension } from "@cheiron/contracts";
import type { Study } from "@cheiron/ctgov";
import { citationExcerpt, citationField, studyLabel } from "./dimensions";

/** Deterministic: start date descending, undated last, NCT id as the tie-break. */
export function mostRecent(studies: Study[], k: number): Study[] {
  if (k <= 0) return [];
  return [...studies]
    .sort((a, b) => (b.start_date ?? "").localeCompare(a.start_date ?? "") || a.nct_id.localeCompare(b.nct_id))
    .slice(0, k);
}

export function citeBucket(studies: Study[], dimension: Dimension, key: string, k: number): Citation[] {
  const field = citationField(dimension);
  return mostRecent(studies, k).map((s) => ({ nct_id: s.nct_id, title: studyLabel(s), field, excerpt: citationExcerpt(s, dimension, key) }));
}

export function citeSelf(s: Study): Citation {
  return { nct_id: s.nct_id, title: studyLabel(s), field: "protocolSection.identificationModule.nctId", excerpt: s.nct_id };
}

const tail = (path: string) => path.split(".").slice(-2).join(".");

export function citeEdge(studies: Study[], a: { field: string; value: string }, b: { field: string; value: string }, k: number): Citation[] {
  return mostRecent(studies, k).map((s) => ({
    nct_id: s.nct_id, title: studyLabel(s), field: `${a.field} | ${b.field}`,
    excerpt: `${tail(a.field)}: "${a.value}" · ${tail(b.field)}: "${b.value}"`,
  }));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Buckets**

`packages/engine/src/aggregate/buckets.ts`:

```ts
import type { Dimension, Granularity, Metric } from "@cheiron/contracts";
import type { Study } from "@cheiron/ctgov";
import { bucketLabel, fixedOrder, isMultiValued, valuesOf } from "../dimensions";

export interface Bucket { key: string; label: string; series: string | null; value: number; studies: Study[] }
export interface Bucketized { buckets: Bucket[]; order: string[]; seriesValues: string[]; omitted: number; withoutValue: number; multiValued: boolean }
export interface BucketizeOptions { dimension: Dimension; secondary?: Dimension | null; metric: Metric; granularity?: Granularity | null; limit?: number | null }

const MAX_SERIES = 8;

export function metricValue(studies: Study[], metric: Metric): number {
  if (metric === "trial_count") return studies.length;
  const xs = studies.map((s) => s.enrollment).filter((n): n is number => n !== null).sort((a, b) => a - b);
  if (!xs.length) return 0;
  if (metric === "enrollment_total") return xs.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
  const mid = Math.floor(xs.length / 2);
  return xs.length % 2 ? xs[mid]! : (xs[mid - 1]! + xs[mid]!) / 2;
}

/** The next period key, for gap filling. */
function nextKey(key: string, g: Granularity): string {
  if (g === "year") return String(Number(key) + 1);
  if (g === "quarter") { const [y, q] = key.split("-Q").map(Number) as [number, number]; return q === 4 ? `${y + 1}-Q1` : `${y}-Q${q + 1}`; }
  const [y, m] = key.split("-").map(Number) as [number, number]; return m === 12 ? `${y + 1}-01` : `${y}-${String(m + 1).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

function fillRange(keys: string[], g: Granularity): string[] {
  if (!keys.length) return [];
  const sorted = [...keys].sort();
  const out: string[] = []; let k = sorted[0]!; const last = sorted[sorted.length - 1]!;
  for (let guard = 0; guard < 2000 && k <= last; guard++) { out.push(k); if (k === last) break; k = nextKey(k, g); }
  return out;
}

/** Primary keys in display order, and how many were dropped by the limit. */
function orderKeys(dimension: Dimension, granularity: Granularity | null, totals: Map<string, number>, limit: number | null): { order: string[]; omitted: number } {
  const present = [...totals.keys()];
  if (dimension === "start_year") return { order: fillRange(present, granularity ?? "year"), omitted: 0 };
  const fixed = fixedOrder(dimension);
  if (fixed) return { order: fixed.filter((k) => totals.has(k)), omitted: 0 };
  const ranked = present.sort((a, b) => (totals.get(b)! - totals.get(a)!) || a.localeCompare(b));
  if (limit !== null && limit !== undefined && ranked.length > limit) return { order: ranked.slice(0, limit), omitted: ranked.length - limit };
  return { order: ranked, omitted: 0 };
}

function orderSeries(secondary: Dimension, totals: Map<string, number>): { values: string[]; omitted: number } {
  const fixed = fixedOrder(secondary);
  if (fixed) return { values: fixed.filter((k) => totals.has(k)), omitted: 0 };
  const ranked = [...totals.keys()].sort((a, b) => (totals.get(b)! - totals.get(a)!) || a.localeCompare(b));
  return { values: ranked.slice(0, MAX_SERIES), omitted: Math.max(0, ranked.length - MAX_SERIES) };
}

const needsEnrollment = (m: Metric) => m !== "trial_count";

export function bucketize(studies: Study[], o: BucketizeOptions): Bucketized {
  const groups = new Map<string, Map<string | null, Study[]>>();
  let withoutValue = 0; let anyMulti = false;
  for (const s of studies) {
    if (needsEnrollment(o.metric) && s.enrollment === null) { withoutValue++; continue; }
    const keys = valuesOf(s, o.dimension, o.granularity ?? "year");
    if (!keys.length) { withoutValue++; continue; }
    if (keys.length > 1) anyMulti = true;
    const seriesKeys: (string | null)[] = o.secondary ? valuesOf(s, o.secondary) : [null];
    if (o.secondary && !seriesKeys.length) { withoutValue++; continue; }
    for (const k of keys) {
      const row = groups.get(k) ?? new Map<string | null, Study[]>();
      for (const sk of seriesKeys) row.set(sk, [...(row.get(sk) ?? []), s]);
      groups.set(k, row);
    }
  }
  const totals = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const [k, row] of groups) totals.set(k, metricValue([...new Set([...row.values()].flat())], o.metric));
  const { order, omitted } = orderKeys(o.dimension, o.granularity ?? null, totals, o.limit ?? null);

  let seriesValues: string[] = []; let seriesOmitted = 0;
  if (o.secondary) {
    const st = new Map<string, number>();
    for (const row of groups.values()) for (const [sk, ss] of row) if (sk !== null) st.set(sk, (st.get(sk) ?? 0) + metricValue(ss, o.metric));
    ({ values: seriesValues, omitted: seriesOmitted } = orderSeries(o.secondary, st));
  }
  const buckets: Bucket[] = [];
  for (const k of order) {
    const row = groups.get(k);
    const label = bucketLabel(o.dimension, k);
    if (!o.secondary) { const ss = row?.get(null) ?? []; buckets.push({ key: k, label, series: null, value: metricValue(ss, o.metric), studies: ss }); continue; }
    for (const sv of seriesValues) { const ss = row?.get(sv) ?? []; buckets.push({ key: k, label, series: sv, value: metricValue(ss, o.metric), studies: ss }); }
  }
  return { buckets, order, seriesValues, omitted: omitted + seriesOmitted, withoutValue, multiValued: isMultiValued(o.dimension) && anyMulti };
}

/** Comparison arms: each arm's buckets become one series, keys unioned and reordered. */
export function mergeArms(parts: { label: string; result: Bucketized }[], o: BucketizeOptions): Bucketized {
  const totals = new Map<string, number>(); const cell = new Map<string, Bucket>();
  for (const { label, result } of parts) for (const b of result.buckets) {
    if (b.series !== null) continue;
    cell.set(`${b.key}\u0000${label}`, { ...b, series: label });
    totals.set(b.key, (totals.get(b.key) ?? 0) + b.value);
  }
  const { order, omitted } = orderKeys(o.dimension, o.granularity ?? null, totals, o.limit ?? null);
  const seriesValues = parts.map((p) => p.label);
  const buckets: Bucket[] = [];
  for (const k of order) for (const sv of seriesValues) buckets.push(cell.get(`${k}\u0000${sv}`) ?? { key: k, label: bucketLabel(o.dimension, k), series: sv, value: 0, studies: [] });
  return { buckets, order, seriesValues, omitted, withoutValue: parts.reduce((n, p) => n + p.result.withoutValue, 0), multiValued: parts.some((p) => p.result.multiValued) };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`packages/engine/src/aggregate/points.ts`:

```ts
import type { Study } from "@cheiron/ctgov";
import { mostRecent } from "../cite";

export interface Point { study: Study; nct_id: string; title: string; start_date: string; enrollment: number; phase: string; series: string | null }

export function scatterPoints(studies: Study[], series: string | null, cap = 500): { points: Point[]; dropped: number; withoutValue: number } {
  const usable = studies.filter((s) => s.start_date !== null && s.enrollment !== null);
  const kept = mostRecent(usable, cap);
  return {
    points: kept.map((s) => ({ study: s, nct_id: s.nct_id, title: s.title || s.nct_id, start_date: s.start_date!, enrollment: s.enrollment!, phase: s.phases[0] ?? "NA", series })),
    dropped: usable.length - kept.length,
    withoutValue: studies.length - usable.length,
  };
}
```

`packages/engine/src/aggregate/graph.ts`:

```ts
import type { EdgeKind } from "@cheiron/contracts";
import type { Study } from "@cheiron/ctgov";
import { conditionIdentity, drugIdentity } from "../dimensions";

export interface GraphNode { id: string; label: string; kind: string; size: number; study_count: number }
export interface GraphEdge { source: string; target: string; weight: number; studies: Study[]; a: { kind: string; value: string }; b: { kind: string; value: string } }

export const EDGE_ENDS: Record<EdgeKind, { a: string; b: string }> = {
  sponsor_drug: { a: "sponsor", b: "drug" }, drug_drug: { a: "drug", b: "drug" }, condition_drug: { a: "condition", b: "drug" },
  sponsor_condition: { a: "sponsor", b: "condition" }, sponsor_collaborator: { a: "sponsor", b: "collaborator" },
};
export const KIND_LABEL: Record<string, string> = { sponsor: "Sponsor", drug: "Drug", condition: "Condition", collaborator: "Collaborator" };
export const KIND_FIELD: Record<string, string> = {
  sponsor: "protocolSection.sponsorCollaboratorsModule.leadSponsor.name",
  drug: "derivedSection.interventionBrowseModule.meshes[].term",
  condition: "derivedSection.conditionBrowseModule.meshes[].term",
  collaborator: "protocolSection.sponsorCollaboratorsModule.collaborators[].name",
};
const PRUNE_ABOVE = 200;

function valuesFor(kind: string, s: Study): string[] {
  switch (kind) {
    case "sponsor": return s.lead_sponsor.name ? [s.lead_sponsor.name] : [];
    case "drug": return drugIdentity(s);
    case "condition": return conditionIdentity(s);
    case "collaborator": return s.collaborators;
    default: return [];
  }
}
const nodeId = (kind: string, value: string) => `${kind}:${value.toLowerCase()}`;

export function buildGraph(studies: Study[], edgeKind: EdgeKind, limit: number): { nodes: GraphNode[]; edges: GraphEdge[]; kinds: { kind: string; label: string }[]; prunedEdges: number } {
  const { a: kindA, b: kindB } = EDGE_ENDS[edgeKind];
  const labels = new Map<string, { label: string; kind: string }>();
  const nodeStudies = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
  const edgeMap = new Map<string, GraphEdge>();
  const touch = (id: string, kind: string, value: string, s: Study) => {
    if (!labels.has(id)) labels.set(id, { label: value, kind });
    const set = nodeStudies.get(id) ?? new Set<string>(); set.add(s.nct_id); nodeStudies.set(id, set);
  };
  for (const s of studies) {
    const pairs: [string, string][] = [];
    if (kindA === kindB) {
      const vs = valuesFor(kindA, s).sort((x, y) => x.localeCompare(y));
      for (let i = 0; i < vs.length; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < vs.length; j++) pairs.push([vs[i]!, vs[j]!]);
    } else {
      for (const va of valuesFor(kindA, s)) for (const vb of valuesFor(kindB, s)) pairs.push([va, vb]);
    }
    for (const [va, vb] of pairs) {
      const ia = nodeId(kindA, va), ib = nodeId(kindB, vb);
      if (ia === ib) continue;
      touch(ia, kindA, va, s); touch(ib, kindB, vb, s);
      const key = `${ia}|${ib}`;
      const e = edgeMap.get(key) ?? { source: ia, target: ib, weight: 0, studies: [], a: { kind: kindA, value: va }, b: { kind: kindB, value: vb } };
      if (!e.studies.some((x) => x.nct_id === s.nct_id)) { e.weight++; e.studies.push(s); }
      edgeMap.set(key, e);
    }
  }
  let edges = [...edgeMap.values()];
  const before = edges.length;
  if (edges.length > PRUNE_ABOVE) edges = edges.filter((e) => e.weight > 1);
  edges.sort((x, y) => y.weight - x.weight || x.source.localeCompare(y.source) || x.target.localeCompare(y.target));
  edges = edges.slice(0, limit);
  const used = new Set(edges.flatMap((e) => [e.source, e.target]));
  const nodes: GraphNode[] = [...used].sort().map((id) => {
    const n = nodeStudies.get(id)!.size; const l = labels.get(id)!;
    return { id, label: l.label, kind: l.kind, size: n, study_count: n };
  });
  const kinds = kindA === kindB ? [{ kind: kindA, label: KIND_LABEL[kindA]! }] : [{ kind: kindA, label: KIND_LABEL[kindA]! }, { kind: kindB, label: KIND_LABEL[kindB]! }];
  return { nodes, edges, kinds, prunedEdges: before - edges.length };
}
```

### Task 13: The spec builder: titles, subtitles, encodings, rows

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/engine/src/spec.ts`, `packages/engine/src/spec.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/engine/src/index.ts` (add `export * from "./spec";`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Plan`, `Bucketized`, `Point`, `GraphNode`, `GraphEdge`, `KIND_FIELD`, `citeBucket`, `citeEdge`, `citeSelf`, `mostRecent`, dimension helpers, `iso3` from `@cheiron/ctgov`.
- Produces:

```ts
export type Aggregate =
  | { kind: "buckets"; result: Bucketized }
  | { kind: "points"; points: Point[]; dropped: number; withoutValue: number }
  | { kind: "graph"; nodes: GraphNode[]; edges: GraphEdge[]; kinds: { kind: string; label: string }[]; prunedEdges: number }
  | { kind: "rows"; studies: Study[] }
  | { kind: "value"; total: number; studies: Study[] };
export interface SpecContext { total_count: number; fetched: number; truncated: boolean; data_timestamp: string; citations_per_datum: number }
export const METRIC_LABEL: Record<Metric, string>;      // "Trials", "Participants enrolled", "Median enrollment"
export const METRIC_UNIT: Record<Metric, "trials" | "participants">;
export const EDGE_LABEL: Record<EdgeKind, string>;      // "Sponsor–drug", ...
export function templateTitle(plan: Plan): string;
export function planSummary(plan: Plan): string;        // one sentence for the titler
export function subtitle(plan: Plan, ctx: SpecContext, agg: Aggregate): string;
export function buildVisualization(plan: Plan, agg: Aggregate, ctx: SpecContext, title: string | null): Visualization;
```

Row conventions (the frontend relies on them): the x cell holds the human label and `<field>_key` the raw key; `series` is always the series field name; the y cell is named after the metric (`trial_count`, `enrollment_total`, `enrollment_median`); time rows use `period`; country rows carry `iso3`; list cells in tables are comma-joined strings.

```ts
import { buildGraph } from "./aggregate/graph";
import { scatterPoints } from "./aggregate/points";
import { buildVisualization, planSummary, subtitle, templateTitle, type Aggregate } from "./spec";
import type { Plan } from "./plan";
import { SAMPLE } from "./test-studies";

const ctx = { total_count: 12, fetched: 12, truncated: false, data_timestamp: "2026-10-08T09:00:05", citations_per_datum: 2 };
const base = (over: Partial<Plan>): Plan => ({
  intent: "distribution", chart: "bar", dimension: "phase", secondary: null, metric: "trial_count", granularity: null, edge_kind: null,
  arms: [{ label: "Pembrolizumab", query: { intr: '"pembrolizumab"' } }], limit: null, recruiting_only: false, notes: [],
  entities: { drugs: ["pembrolizumab"], conditions: [], sponsors: [], countries: [], start_year: null, end_year: null, limit: null },
  interpretation: null as never,
  ...over,
});
const valid = (v: unknown) => { const r = Visualization.safeParse(v); if (!r.success) throw new Error(JSON.stringify(r.error.issues, null, 1)); return r.data; };

describe("titles", () => {
  it("templates by intent", () => {
    expect(templateTitle(base({}))).toBe("Pembrolizumab trials by phase");
    expect(templateTitle(base({ intent: "time_trend", dimension: "start_year", granularity: "year", chart: "line" }))).toBe("Pembrolizumab trials per year");
    expect(templateTitle(base({ intent: "ranking", dimension: "sponsor", limit: 10, entities: { drugs: [], conditions: ["melanoma"], sponsors: [], countries: [], start_year: null, end_year: null, limit: null } }))).toBe("Top 10 sponsors for Melanoma trials");
    expect(templateTitle(base({ intent: "network", dimension: null, edge_kind: "sponsor_drug", chart: "network_graph" }))).toBe("Sponsor–drug network for Pembrolizumab trials");
    expect(templateTitle(base({ intent: "comparison", arms: [{ label: "a", query: {} }, { label: "b", query: {} }] }))).toBe("a vs b: trials by phase");
    expect(templateTitle(base({ intent: "count", chart: "single_value", dimension: null }))).toBe("Pembrolizumab trials");
  });
  it("summarises the plan for the titler", () => {
    expect(planSummary(base({ intent: "time_trend", dimension: "start_year", secondary: "phase", granularity: "year", chart: "line", entities: { drugs: ["pembrolizumab"], conditions: [], sponsors: [], countries: [], start_year: 2015, end_year: null, limit: null } })))
      .toBe("A line chart of the number of trials per year, split by phase, for pembrolizumab, started from 2015.");
  });
  it("subtitle states counts, truncation, counting rule and the data date", () => {
    const agg: Aggregate = { kind: "buckets", result: bucketize(SAMPLE, { dimension: "phase", metric: "trial_count" }) };
    expect(subtitle(base({}), ctx, agg)).toBe("12 trials on ClinicalTrials.gov · counted once per phase · data as of 2026-10-08");
    expect(subtitle(base({ recruiting_only: true }), { ...ctx, total_count: 12480, fetched: 3000, truncated: true }, agg)).toBe("12,480 trials on ClinicalTrials.gov · based on the 3,000 most recently started · recruiting only · counted once per phase · data as of 2026-10-08");
  });
});

describe("buildVisualization", () => {
  it("bar over phases, with labels, keys and citations", () => {
    const v = valid(buildVisualization(base({}), { kind: "buckets", result: bucketize(SAMPLE, { dimension: "phase", metric: "trial_count" }) }, ctx, "Custom title"));
    expect(v.type).toBe("bar"); expect(v.title).toBe("Custom title");
    if (v.type !== "bar") return;
    expect(v.encoding.x).toEqual({ field: "phase", type: "ordinal", label: "Phase", order: ["Early Phase 1", "Phase 1", "Phase 2", "Phase 3", "Not applicable"] });
    expect(v.encoding.y).toEqual({ field: "trial_count", type: "quantitative", label: "Trials", unit: "trials" });
    expect(v.data[2]).toMatchObject({ phase: "Phase 2", phase_key: "PHASE2", trial_count: 4, study_count: 4 });
    expect(v.data[2]!.citations).toHaveLength(2);
  });
  it("ranking is a horizontal, value-sorted bar", () => {
    const v = valid(buildVisualization(base({ intent: "ranking", dimension: "sponsor", limit: 2 }), { kind: "buckets", result: bucketize(SAMPLE, { dimension: "sponsor", metric: "trial_count", limit: 2 }) }, ctx, null));
    if (v.type !== "bar") throw new Error(v.type);
    expect(v.encoding.orientation).toBe("horizontal"); expect(v.encoding.sort).toBe("value_desc"); expect(v.data.map((r) => r.sponsor)).toEqual(["Acme", "Merck"]);
  });
  it("line over periods with a secondary series", () => {
    const r = bucketize(SAMPLE, { dimension: "start_year", secondary: "phase", metric: "trial_count", granularity: "year" });
    const v = valid(buildVisualization(base({ intent: "time_trend", chart: "line", dimension: "start_year", secondary: "phase", granularity: "year" }), { kind: "buckets", result: r }, ctx, null));
    if (v.type !== "line") throw new Error(v.type);
    expect(v.encoding.x).toEqual({ field: "period", type: "temporal", granularity: "year", label: "Start date" });
    expect(v.encoding.series).toEqual({ field: "series", label: "Phase", values: ["Early Phase 1", "Phase 1", "Phase 2", "Phase 3", "Not applicable"] });
    expect(v.data[0]).toMatchObject({ period: "2018", series: "Early Phase 1", trial_count: 0, study_count: 0, citations: [] });
  });
  it("grouped bar from merged arms", () => {
    const a = bucketize(SAMPLE.filter((s) => s.lead_sponsor.name === "Merck"), { dimension: "phase", metric: "trial_count" });
    const b = bucketize(SAMPLE.filter((s) => s.lead_sponsor.name === "Acme"), { dimension: "phase", metric: "trial_count" });
    const m = mergeArms([{ label: "Merck", result: a }, { label: "Acme", result: b }], { dimension: "phase", metric: "trial_count" });
    const v = valid(buildVisualization(base({ intent: "comparison", chart: "grouped_bar", arms: [{ label: "Merck", query: { spons: '"Merck"' } }, { label: "Acme", query: { spons: '"Acme"' } }] }), { kind: "buckets", result: m }, ctx, null));
    if (v.type !== "grouped_bar") throw new Error(v.type);
    expect(v.encoding.series).toEqual({ field: "series", label: "Sponsor", values: ["Merck", "Acme"] });
  });
  it("histogram over enrollment bins", () => {
    const v = valid(buildVisualization(base({ chart: "histogram", dimension: "enrollment" }), { kind: "buckets", result: bucketize(SAMPLE, { dimension: "enrollment", metric: "trial_count" }) }, ctx, null));
    if (v.type !== "histogram") throw new Error(v.type);
    expect(v.encoding.x.bins[0]).toEqual({ label: "1–50", min: 1, max: 50 }); expect(v.encoding.x.bins.at(-1)).toEqual({ label: "1001+", min: 1001, max: null });
  });
  it("map rows carry iso3", () => {
    const v = valid(buildVisualization(base({ intent: "geographic", chart: "map", dimension: "country" }), { kind: "buckets", result: bucketize(SAMPLE, { dimension: "country", metric: "trial_count" }) }, ctx, null));
    if (v.type !== "map") throw new Error(v.type);
    expect(v.data.find((r) => r.country === "Germany")).toMatchObject({ iso3: "DEU", trial_count: 2 });
  });
  it("scatter: one row per trial, citing itself", () => {
    const p = scatterPoints(SAMPLE, null);
    const v = valid(buildVisualization(base({ chart: "scatter" }), { kind: "points", ...p }, ctx, null));
    if (v.type !== "scatter") throw new Error(v.type);
    expect(v.encoding.color).toEqual({ field: "phase", label: "Phase", values: ["Early Phase 1", "Phase 1", "Phase 2", "Phase 3", "Not applicable"] });
    expect(v.data[0]).toMatchObject({ nct_id: "NCT00000012", enrollment: 900, phase: "Phase 3", study_count: 1 });
    expect(v.data[0]!.citations[0]).toMatchObject({ nct_id: "NCT00000012", field: "protocolSection.identificationModule.nctId" });
  });
  it("network: nodes, weighted edges with two-ended citations", () => {
    const g = buildGraph(SAMPLE, "sponsor_drug", 40);
    const v = valid(buildVisualization(base({ intent: "network", chart: "network_graph", dimension: null, edge_kind: "sponsor_drug" }), { kind: "graph", ...g }, ctx, null));
    if (v.type !== "network_graph") throw new Error(v.type);
    const e = v.data.edges.find((x) => x.source === "sponsor:merck" && x.target === "drug:pembrolizumab")!;
    expect(e.weight).toBe(4); expect(e.study_count).toBe(4); expect(e.citations).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(e.citations[0]!.excerpt).toBe('leadSponsor.name: "Merck" · meshes[].term: "pembrolizumab"');
    expect(v.encoding.nodes.kinds).toEqual([{ kind: "sponsor", label: "Sponsor" }, { kind: "drug", label: "Drug" }]);
  });
  it("table and single value", () => {
    const t = valid(buildVisualization(base({ intent: "lookup", chart: "table", dimension: null, limit: 3 }), { kind: "rows", studies: SAMPLE.slice(0, 3) }, ctx, null));
    if (t.type !== "table") throw new Error(t.type);
    expect(t.encoding.columns.map((c) => c.field)).toEqual(["nct_id", "title", "status", "phases", "start_date", "sponsor", "conditions", "enrollment"]);
    expect(t.data[0]).toMatchObject({ nct_id: "NCT00000001", phases: "Phase 3", study_count: 1 });
    const s = valid(buildVisualization(base({ intent: "count", chart: "single_value", dimension: null }), { kind: "value", total: 2973, studies: SAMPLE }, ctx, null));
    if (s.type !== "single_value") throw new Error(s.type);
    expect(s.data[0]).toMatchObject({ value: 2973, label: "trials", study_count: 2973 }); expect(s.data[0]!.citations).toHaveLength(2);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`packages/engine/src/spec.ts`:

```ts
import type { Citation, DataRow, EdgeKind, Metric, Visualization } from "@cheiron/contracts";
import { iso3, type Study } from "@cheiron/ctgov";
import type { Bucketized } from "./aggregate/buckets";
import { KIND_FIELD, type GraphEdge, type GraphNode } from "./aggregate/graph";
import type { Point } from "./aggregate/points";
import { citeBucket, citeEdge, citeSelf, mostRecent } from "./cite";
import { bucketLabel, conditionIdentity, DIMENSION_LABEL, DIMENSION_LABEL_PLURAL, ENROLLMENT_BINS, fixedOrder } from "./dimensions";
import { entityLabel, type Plan } from "./plan";

export type Aggregate =
  | { kind: "buckets"; result: Bucketized }
  | { kind: "points"; points: Point[]; dropped: number; withoutValue: number }
  | { kind: "graph"; nodes: GraphNode[]; edges: GraphEdge[]; kinds: { kind: string; label: string }[]; prunedEdges: number }
  | { kind: "rows"; studies: Study[] }
  | { kind: "value"; total: number; studies: Study[] };
export interface SpecContext { total_count: number; fetched: number; truncated: boolean; data_timestamp: string; citations_per_datum: number }

export const METRIC_LABEL: Record<Metric, string> = { trial_count: "Trials", enrollment_total: "Participants enrolled", enrollment_median: "Median enrollment" };
export const METRIC_UNIT: Record<Metric, "trials" | "participants"> = { trial_count: "trials", enrollment_total: "participants", enrollment_median: "participants" };
export const EDGE_LABEL: Record<EdgeKind, string> = { sponsor_drug: "Sponsor–drug", drug_drug: "Drug–drug", condition_drug: "Condition–drug", sponsor_condition: "Sponsor–condition", sponsor_collaborator: "Sponsor–collaborator" };
const METRIC_PHRASE: Record<Metric, string> = { trial_count: "the number of trials", enrollment_total: "the total enrollment", enrollment_median: "the median enrollment" };
const fmt = (n: number) => n.toLocaleString("en-US");
const singular = (plural: string) => plural.replace(/ies$/, "y").replace(/ses$/, "s").replace(/s$/, "");

export function templateTitle(p: Plan): string {
  const named = entityLabel(p.entities) !== "All trials";
  const e = named ? entityLabel(p.entities) : "All";          // "All trials by phase", not "All trials trials by phase"
  const forE = named ? ` for ${e} trials` : "";
  const dim = p.dimension ? DIMENSION_LABEL[p.dimension].toLowerCase() : "";
  const sec = p.secondary ? ` and ${DIMENSION_LABEL[p.secondary].toLowerCase()}` : "";
  const measure = p.metric === "trial_count" ? "trials" : METRIC_LABEL[p.metric].toLowerCase();
  switch (p.intent) {
    case "time_trend": return `${e} ${measure} per ${p.granularity ?? "year"}${p.secondary ? ` by ${DIMENSION_LABEL[p.secondary].toLowerCase()}` : ""}`;
    case "distribution": return `${e} ${measure} by ${dim}${sec}`;
    case "comparison": return `${p.arms.map((a) => a.label).join(" vs ")}: ${measure} by ${dim}`;
    case "ranking": return `Top ${p.limit ?? 10} ${p.dimension ? DIMENSION_LABEL_PLURAL[p.dimension] : "categories"}${forE}`;
    case "geographic": return `${e} ${measure} by country`;
    case "network": return `${p.edge_kind ? EDGE_LABEL[p.edge_kind] : "Relationship"} network${forE}`;
    case "count": case "lookup": case "other": return `${e} trials`;
  }
}

/** One sentence the titler can work from; it names the chart, the measure, the split, the entities and the window. */
export function planSummary(p: Plan): string {
  const chart = p.chart.replace("_", " ");
  const measure = METRIC_PHRASE[p.metric];
  const parts: string[] = [];
  if (p.intent === "time_trend") parts.push(`${measure} per ${p.granularity ?? "year"}`);
  else if (p.intent === "network") parts.push(`which ${p.edge_kind ? EDGE_LABEL[p.edge_kind].toLowerCase() : "entity"} pairs co-occur`);
  else if (p.intent === "count") parts.push(measure);
  else if (p.intent === "lookup") parts.push("a list of trials");
  else parts.push(`${measure} by ${p.dimension ? DIMENSION_LABEL[p.dimension].toLowerCase() : "category"}`);
  if (p.secondary) parts.push(`split by ${DIMENSION_LABEL[p.secondary].toLowerCase()}`);
  if (p.intent === "comparison") parts.push(`comparing ${p.arms.map((a) => a.label).join(" and ")}`);
  const named = [...p.entities.drugs, ...p.entities.conditions, ...p.entities.sponsors, ...p.entities.countries];
  if (named.length && p.intent !== "comparison") parts.push(`for ${named.join(", ")}`);
  if (p.entities.start_year !== null && p.entities.end_year !== null) parts.push(`started ${p.entities.start_year} to ${p.entities.end_year}`);
  else if (p.entities.start_year !== null) parts.push(`started from ${p.entities.start_year}`);
  else if (p.entities.end_year !== null) parts.push(`started up to ${p.entities.end_year}`);
  if (p.recruiting_only) parts.push("recruiting only");
  return `A ${chart} chart of ${parts.join(", ")}.`;
}

export function subtitle(p: Plan, ctx: SpecContext, agg: Aggregate): string {
  const parts = [`${fmt(ctx.total_count)} trials on ClinicalTrials.gov`];
  if (ctx.truncated) parts.push(`based on the ${fmt(ctx.fetched)} most recently started`);
  if (p.recruiting_only) parts.push("recruiting only");
  if (agg.kind === "buckets" && agg.result.multiValued && p.dimension) parts.push(`counted once per ${singular(DIMENSION_LABEL_PLURAL[p.dimension])}`);
  if (agg.kind === "buckets" && agg.result.omitted > 0 && p.dimension) parts.push(`${fmt(agg.result.omitted)} more ${DIMENSION_LABEL_PLURAL[p.dimension]} not shown`);
  if (agg.kind === "points" && agg.dropped > 0) parts.push(`${fmt(agg.points.length)} most recent trials shown`);
  if (agg.kind === "graph" && agg.prunedEdges > 0) parts.push(`${fmt(agg.edges.length)} strongest links shown`);
  parts.push(`data as of ${ctx.data_timestamp.slice(0, 10)}`);
  return parts.join(" · ");
}

const yAxis = (m: Metric) => ({ field: m, type: "quantitative" as const, label: METRIC_LABEL[m], unit: METRIC_UNIT[m] });
const seriesLabel = (p: Plan): string => p.secondary ? DIMENSION_LABEL[p.secondary] : p.intent === "comparison" ? comparisonAxisLabel(p) : "Series";
function comparisonAxisLabel(p: Plan): string {
  const q = p.arms[0]?.query ?? {};
  if (q.intr) return "Drug"; if (q.cond) return "Condition"; if (q.spons) return "Sponsor"; if (q.locn) return "Country";
  const e = p.entities;
  if (e.drugs.length >= 2) return "Drug"; if (e.conditions.length >= 2) return "Condition"; if (e.sponsors.length >= 2) return "Sponsor"; if (e.countries.length >= 2) return "Country";
  return "Arm";
}
const seriesValueLabel = (p: Plan, v: string) => (p.secondary ? bucketLabel(p.secondary, v) : v);

export function buildVisualization(p: Plan, agg: Aggregate, ctx: SpecContext, title: string | null): Visualization {
  const head = { title: title?.trim() || templateTitle(p), subtitle: subtitle(p, ctx, agg) };
  const k = ctx.citations_per_datum;

  if (agg.kind === "buckets") {
    const r = agg.result; const dim = p.dimension ?? "phase"; const m = p.metric;
    const rowOf = (b: typeof r.buckets[number], extra: Record<string, string | number | null> = {}): DataRow => ({
      ...extra, ...(b.series !== null ? { series: seriesValueLabel(p, b.series) } : {}), [m]: b.value, study_count: b.studies.length,
      citations: citeBucket(b.studies, dim, b.key, k),
    });
    const series = r.seriesValues.length ? { field: "series", label: seriesLabel(p), values: r.seriesValues.map((v) => seriesValueLabel(p, v)) } : undefined;
    if (p.chart === "line") {
      return { type: "line", ...head, encoding: { x: { field: "period", type: "temporal", granularity: p.granularity ?? "year", label: "Start date" }, y: yAxis(m), ...(series ? { series } : {}) }, data: r.buckets.map((b) => rowOf(b, { period: b.key })) };
    }
    if (p.chart === "histogram") {
      const bins = dim === "enrollment" ? ENROLLMENT_BINS.filter((bin) => r.order.includes(bin.label)) : r.order.map((key) => ({ label: key, min: Number(key), max: Number(key) }));
      return { type: "histogram", ...head, encoding: { x: { field: dim, type: "ordinal", label: DIMENSION_LABEL[dim], bins }, y: yAxis(m) }, data: r.buckets.map((b) => rowOf(b, { [dim]: b.label, [`${dim}_key`]: b.key })) };
    }
    if (p.chart === "map") {
      return { type: "map", ...head, encoding: { geo: { field: "country", code_field: "iso3", level: "country" }, value: { field: m, label: METRIC_LABEL[m], unit: METRIC_UNIT[m] } }, data: r.buckets.map((b) => rowOf(b, { country: b.key, iso3: iso3(b.key) })) };
    }
    const xField = dim === "start_year" ? "period" : dim;
    const ordinal = dim === "start_year" || fixedOrder(dim) !== null;
    const ranked = p.intent === "ranking" || (!ordinal && p.intent !== "comparison");
    const x = { field: xField, type: ordinal ? ("ordinal" as const) : ("nominal" as const), label: DIMENSION_LABEL[dim], order: r.order.map((key) => bucketLabel(dim, key)) };
    const encoding = { x, y: yAxis(m), orientation: ranked ? ("horizontal" as const) : ("vertical" as const), sort: ranked ? ("value_desc" as const) : ("x_order" as const) };
    const data = r.buckets.map((b) => rowOf(b, { [xField]: b.label, [`${xField}_key`]: b.key, ...(dim === "country" ? { iso3: iso3(b.key) } : {}) }));
    if (p.chart === "grouped_bar" && series) return { type: "grouped_bar", ...head, encoding: { ...encoding, series }, data };
    if (p.chart === "stacked_bar" && series) return { type: "stacked_bar", ...head, encoding: { ...encoding, series }, data };
    return { type: "bar", ...head, encoding, data };
  }

  if (agg.kind === "points") {
    const phases = fixedOrder("phase")!.filter((ph) => agg.points.some((pt) => pt.phase === ph)).map((ph) => bucketLabel("phase", ph));
    const multi = p.arms.length > 1;
    return {
      type: "scatter", ...head,
      encoding: {
        x: { field: "start_date", type: "temporal", label: "Start date" }, y: { field: "enrollment", type: "quantitative", label: "Enrollment", unit: "participants" },
        color: { field: "phase", label: "Phase", values: phases }, ...(multi ? { series: { field: "series", label: comparisonAxisLabel(p), values: p.arms.map((a) => a.label) } } : {}), point_id: "nct_id",
      },
      data: agg.points.map((pt) => ({ nct_id: pt.nct_id, title: pt.title, start_date: pt.start_date, enrollment: pt.enrollment, phase: bucketLabel("phase", pt.phase), ...(multi ? { series: pt.series ?? "" } : {}), study_count: 1, citations: [citeSelf(pt.study)] })),
    };
  }

  if (agg.kind === "graph") {
    return {
      type: "network_graph", ...head,
      encoding: { nodes: { id_field: "id", label_field: "label", kind_field: "kind", size_field: "size", kinds: agg.kinds }, edges: { source_field: "source", target_field: "target", weight_field: "weight" }, layout: "force" },
      data: {
        nodes: agg.nodes.map((n) => ({ id: n.id, label: n.label, kind: n.kind, size: n.size, study_count: n.study_count })),
        edges: agg.edges.map((e) => ({ source: e.source, target: e.target, weight: e.weight, study_count: e.weight, citations: citeEdge(e.studies, { field: KIND_FIELD[e.a.kind]!, value: e.a.value }, { field: KIND_FIELD[e.b.kind]!, value: e.b.value }, k) })),
      },
    };
  }

  if (agg.kind === "rows") {
    return {
      type: "table", ...head,
      encoding: {
        columns: [
          { field: "nct_id", label: "NCT ID", type: "string" }, { field: "title", label: "Title", type: "string" }, { field: "status", label: "Status", type: "string" },
          { field: "phases", label: "Phases", type: "list" }, { field: "start_date", label: "Start", type: "date" }, { field: "sponsor", label: "Lead sponsor", type: "string" },
          { field: "conditions", label: "Conditions", type: "list" }, { field: "enrollment", label: "Enrollment", type: "number" },
        ],
        sort: { field: "start_date", direction: "desc" },
      },
      data: agg.studies.map((s) => ({
        nct_id: s.nct_id, title: s.title || s.nct_id, status: bucketLabel("status", s.status), phases: s.phases.map((ph) => bucketLabel("phase", ph)).join(", "),
        start_date: s.start_date, sponsor: s.lead_sponsor.name, conditions: conditionIdentity(s).join(", "), enrollment: s.enrollment, study_count: 1, citations: [citeSelf(s)],
      })),
    };
  }

  const citations: Citation[] = mostRecent(agg.studies, k).map(citeSelf);
  return { type: "single_value", ...head, encoding: { value: { field: "value", label: METRIC_LABEL[p.metric], unit: METRIC_UNIT[p.metric] } }, data: [{ value: agg.total, label: "trials", study_count: agg.total, citations }] };
}
```

```ts
// openai.ts
export const EXTRACTOR_MODEL = "gpt-5.4-nano";    // Task 21 may change this constant after the benchmark
export const TITLER_MODEL = "gpt-5.4-nano";
export type OpenAiFailure = "missing_api_key" | "openai_timeout" | "openai_network" | "openai_http" | "openai_incomplete" | "openai_invalid_json" | "openai_invalid_shape";
export interface OpenAiEnv { OPENAI_API_KEY?: string; OPENAI_BASE_URL?: string; EXTRACTOR_MODEL?: string; TITLER_MODEL?: string }
export function responsesCall(opts: { env: OpenAiEnv; http?: typeof fetch; model: string; system: string; user: string; schema?: { name: string; schema: Record<string, unknown> }; maxOutputTokens: number; timeoutMs: number; signal?: AbortSignal }): Promise<{ ok: true; text: string; ms: number } | { ok: false; reason: OpenAiFailure; detail?: string; ms: number }>;
// extract.ts
export type Extraction = { ok: true; entities: Entities; ms: number } | { ok: false; reason: OpenAiFailure; detail?: string; ms: number };
export type Extractor = (query: string, today: string, signal?: AbortSignal) => Promise<Extraction>;
export const ENTITIES_SCHEMA: Record<string, unknown>;
export function parseEntities(text: string): Entities | null;
export function openAiExtractor(env: OpenAiEnv, http?: typeof fetch): Extractor;
export function fakeExtractor(entities: Partial<Entities>): Extractor;
// title.ts

export function openAiTitler(env: OpenAiEnv, http?: typeof fetch): Titler;
export function fakeTitler(title: string | null): Titler;
```
